# Bug audit — 2026-10-04 (checkpoint)

**Area:** the `packages/ui` components that start with C: Calendar, Card, Checkbox, Code, Collapse, Color, Combobox, CommandPalette, Confirm, ContextMenu, Control, CopyButton, CreditCardInput, and CurrencyInput, with the kata recipes they read. The `chart` and `chat` modules and the `container` structure are out of the area. 7 scopes, 118 files, 10,425 lines, 150 claims; 137 findings stay open.

**State:** checkpoint at the request of the owner, before the session limit. This file replaces the paused record of commit a745b7f1a.

- Done: the sweeps (C1–C7, every file read), the blind verification of all 150 claims (the evidence stripped by code; no claim UNLOCATED), and the overturn pass of each scope with the sweep evidence and the caller checks.
- Pending: the merge pass across the seven sheets (bug-verifier §3.15), the record in the bug-audit format (`.claude/commands/bug-audit.md` §4), the open questions to the owner, the settle pass, and the fixes.
- A container restart stopped the first overturn passes before they reported; all seven ran again from the same inputs.

**Counts after the overturn pass:** 117 CONFIRMED, 19 NARROWED, 1 RESTATED, 13 REFUTED. Severity of the open findings: 1 high, 15 medium, 121 low.

## Caller runtime checks

A Chromium probe ran under the `packages/ui` Vitest browser config, and Node 22 with ICU 77.1 checked locale data. A cell that names only the engine means that the probe reproduced the claim. The overturn passes received these cells as written and read a bare "Chromium" as a check with no outcome. A reproduction cannot show a claim deliberate or known, so no verdict depends on that reading.

| Row | Check |
|---|---|
| C1-C07 | Chromium: 34 Tab presses cross one calendar |
| C1-C08 | Chromium |
| C1-C14 | Chromium: the RTL start keeps its band-side corners round |
| C2-C19 | Chromium, rerun in the `floating-ui` project (live floating-ui, a trusted CDP key from `vitest/browser`, `IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT` false, focus on the combobox input, a Drawer whose `onOpenChange` writes state): one Escape closed the panel and the Drawer (`onOpenChange(false)` fired once), with and without a typed query. Reproduced. The first probe ran in the mocked `browser` project and did not reproduce. |

| C3-C02 | Chromium |
| C4-C01 | Chromium |
| C4-C20 | Chromium: at 1280×720 the confirm button is at 2048px, with no scroll container |

| C5-C19 | Chromium: hover color `oklch(0.141 …)` |

| C6-C06 | Chromium, rerun on the box `[data-slot="control"]`: a checked blue box at rest has border `oklab(0.424 -0.0151 -0.1984 / 0.9)`; a box hover or a label hover turns it to `oklab(0.141 … / 0.7)` in light mode and `oklab(0.99999 … / 0.5)` in dark mode. The first probe read the overlay input, which has no border rule, and did not reproduce. |

|---|---|---|
| `hooks/use-escape-layer.ts` | Escape that cancels an IME composition dismisses the top layer, because the layer does not check `isComposing`. | C2-C12, C4-C12 |
| `hooks/a11y/use-a11y-roving.ts` | The virtual seed does not read `isDisabled` and does not scroll the seeded row. `handleActivationKey` drops the key modifiers. `clearVirtualActive` leaves `data-active` on rows that stay mounted while the panel exits. | C2-C13, C2-C20, C4-C08, C4-C11, C4-C13 |
| `primitives/overlay/overlay.tsx` | The exiting overlay takes input for its whole exit animation; FloatingSurface does not. | C4-C02, C4-C18 |
| `components/menu/use-menu-state.ts` | A context menu has no trigger ref, and `openAt` records no focus to restore. | C4-C24 |
| `components/menu/menu.tsx` | `className` lands on a `display: contents` root. | C4-C25 |
| `recipes/kiso/hannou/active.ts` | The `data-active` wash has no glass form and no forced-colors form. | C4-C14, C4-C15 |
| `hooks/observe-scroll-extent.ts` | It measures again only when the node or a direct child resizes, or when the node's children change. | C5-C04 |
| `hooks/use-scroll-overflow.ts` | It does not measure again when a late web font loads. | — |
| `hooks/use-scroll-region.ts` | `scrollsOn` has no 1px tolerance, so a box that fits can become a region. | — |
| `core/scroll/fade.ts` | The fade mask hides the inset focus ring of a scroll rail at its ends. | — |
| `recipes/kata/toggle-icon-button.ts` | `flex` wins over the `inline-flex` of Button, so CopyButton and ToggleIconButton break onto their own line in text. | — |
| `utilities/digits-only.ts` | `/\D/g` has no `u` flag, so it strips Unicode digits. | C7-C12, C7-C25 |
| `hooks/use-pending-caret.ts` | `setSelectionRange` throws on an input type with no selection, such as `number`. | C7-C08, C7-C18, C7-C21 |

| `recipes/kiso/narabi/toggle.ts` | The first column is a fixed 18px, which is narrower than the stepped box at `lg`. | C6-C05 |

| `components/switch/switch.tsx` | Switch ignores `readOnly`, and `style` and `hidden` go to the invisible input. | C6-C01, C6-C03 |

Every other surviving claim has reach NONE and is rated low.

##### §4.1 Records

###### C1-C01
- **verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - The preview becomes the effective end. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-range.tsx:150` `const effectiveEnd = hoverDate ?? rangeEnd`
  - With no start, that end still counts as an edge. `:66-68` `const isEdge =` … `(effectiveEnd != null && isSameDay(date, effectiveEnd))`
  - The early return keeps it. `:70-71` `if (rangeStart == null || effectiveEnd == null) {` / `return { isEdge, isInnerRange: false, isLeftEdge: false, isRightEdge: false }`
  - `getDayProps` selects it. `:163-165` `selected: isEdge,` / `variant: isInnerRange ? 'soft' : undefined,` / `color: isInnerRange ? 'blue' : undefined,`
  - The grid takes that flag. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-grid.tsx:95` `const selected = dayProps?.selected ?? isSelected`
  - The cell paints it as committed:
    - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-day-cell.tsx:61` `aria-selected={selected}`
    - `:64` `variant={variant ?? (selected ? 'solid' : isToday ? 'soft' : 'plain')}`
    - `:65` `color={color ?? (selected || isToday ? 'blue' : undefined)}`
  - The listbox is multiselectable. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-range.tsx:189` `multiselectable` → `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-grid.tsx:74` `aria-multiselectable={ariaAttr(multiselectable)}`
- **trigger:** As stated.
- **intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-range.tsx:36` "Day under the pointer, used as the provisional end of the band before the second click commits `rangeEnd`." This scopes the prop to the time between the two clicks. It says nothing about a preview with no start.
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-range.test.tsx:90-93` pins a selected hover end only when a start exists: `renderUI(<CalendarRange rangeStart={d(2024, 3, 5)} hoverDate={d(2024, 3, 8)} />)` / `expect(findDay(8)).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true')`.
  - No source makes the no-start case deliberate.
- **reach:** NONE.
  - No root calls `CalendarRange` directly (search `\bCalendar(Range|Skeleton)?\b` in apps, demos, modules, layouts).
  - The only range route is `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/filters.tsx:136` `<DatePicker range />`. DatePicker is out-of-root and never sends a preview without a start:
    - `packages/ui/src/components/date-picker/use-date-picker-range-state.ts:262` `rangeStart: rangeStart ?? (value ? value[0] : null),`
    - `:264` `hoverDate: rangeStart !== null ? hoverDate : null,`
- **severity:** low. No shipped surface builds this state, and the cost is one day announced as selected while the pointer rests on it.
- **group:** independent
- **priorArt:** NONE (terms: CalendarRange, hover, hoverDate, aria-selected, range).
- **dependency:** none (no node_modules citation).

###### C1-C02
- **verdict:** NARROWED
- **mechanism:**
  - With no `size`, the skeleton writes no scope and takes the nearest one. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-skeleton.tsx:32` `<div data-density={size} aria-hidden="true" className={cn(k.base, 'relative', className)}>`
  - Its header row and day rows use the button height:
    - `:35` `<div className={cn(k.header, k.skeleton.row)} />`
    - `:43` `<div key={rowKey} className={cn('col-span-7', k.skeleton.row)} />`
    - Chain: `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/calendar.ts:63` `skeleton: kokkaku.calendar,` → `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/calendar.ts:22` `row: button.height,` → `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/button.ts:18` `const height = dan.size.button.base` → `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/dan/size.ts:67` `base: 'density-h-[5.5,7.5,9.5,11.5,11.5]',`
    - That is 22px at xs and 30px at sm.
  - The width and the header margin are three-value ramps, so xs equals sm:
    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/dan/size.ts:144` `width: 'density-w-[52,68,80]',`
    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/dan/space.ts:185` `bottom: 'density-mb-[1,2,3]',`
    - `packages/ui/src/core/density/steps.ts:109` `return { xs: sm, sm, md, lg, xl: lg }`
  - The weekday row is square on the column width, so it matches. `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/calendar.ts:34` `'w-full aspect-square',`
  - The calendar snaps xs to sm:
    - The scale has no xs step: `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/calendar.ts:67-72` builds it from four three-value ramps.
    - `packages/ui/src/core/density/scale.ts:96` `return (scale as readonly DensityStep[]).includes(step) ? (step as S) : (toInnerStep(step) as S)`
    - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx:192` `const step = useDensityStep(size)`
    - `:196` `const density = snapped === step ? size : snapped`
    - `:289` `<Box data-slot="calendar" density={density} …>`
    - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar.test.tsx:288-296` pins the sm scope inside `<Box density="xs">`.
  - Result: seven rows (header plus six day rows) × 8px = 56px.
- **trigger (verified):** An xs scope opened through a `density` prop (for example `<Box density="xs">`) around a Suspense fallback or a `loading.tsx` that shows `<CalendarSkeleton />` with no `size`. It resolves to `<Calendar />` showing a six-week month.
  - A client conditional render also shifts. But `CONVENTIONS.md:41` directs that swap to ReadyReveal: "When the client swaps a skeleton for the real component, use `ReadyReveal`, not a conditional render."
  - Three routes are ruled out (see the sheet): ReadyReveal, a root step of xs, and a raw `data-density` attribute.
- **intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-skeleton.tsx:23-24` "six day rows come from the calendar recipes, so the block has the box of a" / "real calendar at each step."
  - `packages/ui/REFERENCE.md:32` "A skeleton follows density as its component does."
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/geometry/skeleton-parity.test.tsx:797` `it.each(['sm', 'md', 'lg'] as const)('CalendarSkeleton has the box of a %s calendar', (size) => {` covers explicit steps only.
  - Nothing makes the xs gap deliberate.
- **reach:** NONE.
  - The one root site, `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/primitives/skeleton.tsx:65` `{ name: 'Calendar', skeleton: <CalendarSkeleton /> },`, renders alone (`:140` `{active?.skeleton}`). It has no xs scope and no swap.
  - No app renders `CalendarSkeleton`.
  - Test-only sites: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/a11y/cases/inputs.tsx:244` and `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/geometry/skeleton-parity.test.tsx:801`.
- **severity:** low. No shipped surface puts the skeleton in an xs scope, and the cost is one 56px shift at a server swap.
- **group:** independent
- **priorArt:** NONE (terms: skeleton, density, xs, ReadyReveal, layout shift).
- **dependency:** none.

###### C1-C03
- **verdict:** NARROWED
- **mechanism:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:45-46` `export function fromCalendarDate(date: CalendarDate): Date {` / `return date.toDate(getLocalTimeZone())`
  - `toDate` hands off to the conversion module:
    - `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/dist/private/CalendarDate.mjs:81-82` `toDate(timeZone) {` / `return (0, $d07e34cce18680fd$export$e67a095c620b86fe)(this, timeZone);`
    - `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/dist/private/conversion.mjs:138-139` `…(dateTime, timeZone, disambiguation = 'compatible') {` / `return new Date($d07e34cce18680fd$export$5107c82f94518f5c(dateTime, timeZone, disambiguation));`
  - A UTC zone returns UTC midnight. `conversion.mjs:99` `if (timeZone === 'UTC') return $d07e34cce18680fd$export$bd4fb2bc8bb06fb(dateTime);`
  - An override skips the native path. `conversion.mjs:101-102` "Skip this fast path if the local timezone was explicitly overridden via setLocalTimeZone, since native Date always uses the browser's timezone, not the overridden one."
  - The setter records the override. `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/dist/private/queries.mjs:95-97` `function $ad063034c8620db8$export$61a9d83ceb59a3dd(timeZone) {` / `$ad063034c8620db8$var$localTimeZoneOverride = true;` / `$ad063034c8620db8$var$localTimeZone = timeZone;`
  - Every read in the calendar uses the runtime zone's local getters:
    - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-month.ts:17` `return firstOfMonth(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth())`
    - `:50` `const index = month.getFullYear() * 12 + month.getMonth() + delta`
    - `:102-104` year and month come from `viewDate.getFullYear()` / `viewDate.getMonth()`
  - Traced in America/New_York after `setLocalTimeZone('UTC')`:
    - June seeds `firstOfMonth(2025, 5)` = 2025-06-01T00:00Z = May 31, 20:00 local. The view is May, and the header reads "May 2025" (`packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:144` formats in the runtime zone).
    - The cells run Apr 30 to May 30, so the `getDate() === 1` column offset (`packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-grid.tsx:97`) lands on the second cell.
    - `nextMonth` gives the same instant, so the view does not move. `prevMonth` gives Mar 31 local, so it lands on March.
    - `:164` `formatter.format(firstOfMonth(2021, index))` gives "Dec, Jan, …".
    - `:154` gives a weekday row that starts on Sat.
  - Every bullet of the stated wrong result traces.
- **trigger (verified):** An app calls `setLocalTimeZone` with a zone east of the runtime zone (`'UTC'` in New York qualifies), then renders `<Calendar />`. The OS-zone-change route is ruled out (see the sheet).
- **intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:38` "Inverse of `toCalendarDate`: a local-midnight `Date` for the calendar day."
  - `:27-28` "This mirrors the wall-clock-day semantics the calendar uses everywhere (`getFullYear`/`getMonth`/`getDate`)".
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-utilities.test.ts:71-77` asserts local midnight with no override.
  - Not deliberate.
- **reach:** NONE.
  - Search `setLocalTimeZone|resetLocalTimeZone` in apps (excluding node_modules), demos, modules, and layouts: no match.
  - No app declares the package (search `internationalized` in apps: no match).
- **severity:** low. No shipped surface sets an override. If one did, the cost would be high: every label and both chevrons go wrong.
- **group:** independent
- **priorArt:** NONE (terms: time zone, getLocalTimeZone, setLocalTimeZone, fromCalendarDate).
- **dependency:**
  - Package: `@internationalized/date` 3.12.2. `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/package.json:3` `"version": "3.12.2",` and `:22` `"import": "./dist/index.mjs",`.
  - `pnpm-workspace.yaml:47-50`: overrides cover esbuild, postcss, and undici only. `:64-65`: patchedDependencies lists `'@floating-ui/react@0.27.19'` only. None applies.
  - The root package.json has no `pnpm` block. I did not list `patches/`; pnpm applies only the patches it registers.
  - `tsconfig.base.json:19-20` `"paths": {` / `"@/*": ["./src/*"]` — none applies. The packages/ui tsconfig has no paths.
  - No `resolve.alias` in `vitest.config.ts`, `vitest.base.ts`, `vitest.compiler.config.ts`, `vite.fixtures.config.ts`, or `vite.docs.config.ts` (which delegates to `packages/ui/src/docs/engine/vite/index.ts`). The package appears only under `optimizeDeps.include` (`vitest.browser.config.ts:217`, `packages/ui/src/docs/engine/vite/index.ts:107`); that is pre-bundling, not an alias.
  - Vendored copy: I did not search `packages/ui/src`, because that search reaches `__benchmarks__`. The scope imports the bare specifier (`packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:7` `} from '@internationalized/date'`), and no surface maps it, so the build resolves the node_modules copy.

###### C1-C05
- **verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx:198` `const localeTag = resolveLocale(locale ?? ambient.locale)`
  - Outside a provider, `ambient.locale` is undefined. `packages/ui/src/providers/locale/context.ts:28-29` `…createContext<LocaleConfig>('Locale', {` / `default: {},`
  - Each side then reads its own runtime locale:
    - `packages/ui/src/utilities/resolve-locale.ts:11` `const runtimeLocale = once(() => new Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().locale)`
    - `:21` `return locale ?? runtimeLocale()`
  - The weekday labels follow that locale:
    - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx:263` `const weekdays = useMemo(() => getWeekdayLabels(localeTag), [localeTag])`
    - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:149` `const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { weekday: 'short' })`
    - `:151` `const start = startOfWeek(WEEKDAY_REFERENCE, locale)`
  - The weekday row is in the server markup even with no seed. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx:161-162` "The server and the hydration render draw the header and the weekday row, with no month label and no days."
  - A seeded calendar also server-renders `:272` `formatMonthName(viewDate, localeTag)` and `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-day-cell.tsx:62` `aria-label={formatDayName(date, localeTag)}`.
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-hydration.test.tsx:19-20` "A text or a node mismatch reaches `onRecoverableError`, and React logs an attribute mismatch to `consoleError`."
- **trigger:** As stated. For the docs, the build machine's Node default stands in for the server's.
- **intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx:124-126` "Resolution order: explicit prop, then enclosing `LocaleProvider`, then the runtime default." This documents the fallback, not agreement between server and client.
  - `:155-157` covers "today" only.
  - `packages/ui/src/utilities/resolve-locale.ts:6` "while the document's locale is fixed for the process". The server and the browser are two processes.
  - `packages/ui/REFERENCE.md:37` "`locale` needs an API decision — explicit props or app wrappers".
  - The hydration tests render both phases in one process (`packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-hydration.test.tsx:22-48`), so they cannot see this.
  - Not deliberate.
- **reach:**
  - The docs site prerenders each page in Node and the browser hydrates it. These files are out-of-root, cited for the render path:
    - `packages/ui/src/docs/react-router.config.ts:20-21` "A static site: no server at run time. The build renders each page to its own" / "HTML file, and the browser hydrates it."
    - `:26` `ssr: false,` and `:27` `prerender: () => ['/', ...pagePaths()],`
    - `packages/ui/src/docs/app/entry.server.tsx:6` "The build renders each page once, to a static file. `prerender` waits for all content."
  - No LocaleProvider wraps the demos: `packages/ui/src/docs/app/root.tsx` has none; `packages/ui/src/docs/engine/app.tsx:97-98` has `<UIProvider link={RouterLink}>` / `<AppearanceProvider>`; `packages/ui/src/providers/ui` holds no locale.
  - In-root sites:
    - `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/calendar.tsx:11` `<Calendar value={date} onValueChange={setDate} />`
    - `:34` `<Axes of="Calendar" omit={['multiselectable']} render={(props) => <Calendar {...props} />} />`
    - `:39` `<Calendar min={min} max={max} />`
  - Apps: NONE. The calendar sits in the portaled DatePicker dialog (`packages/ui/src/components/date-picker/date-picker-content.tsx:85` "Portaled, animated modal dialog shell for the picker popover."), which the server does not render open.
- **severity:** medium. Every docs reader whose browser locale differs from the build machine's hits it on each calendar demo page. React heals it with a client re-render.
- **group:** G1
- **priorArt:** NONE (terms: locale, LocaleProvider, hydration, onRecoverableError, prerender).

The calendar matches neither exception.

###### C1-C08
- **verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**

    - `:88` `return currentIndex + cols < itemCount ? currentIndex + cols : currentIndex % cols` takes June 28 (index 27) to index 6, which is June 7.

The view moves only when `activeGridDate` changes (`packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-month.ts:128-139`), and a standalone calendar has none (`packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx:213` `const activeGridDate = active?.zone === 'grid' ? active.date : null`).

  - `:274-275` "Month navigation (header chevrons, picker, arrowing across a boundary)".

  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/use-calendar-focus.test.ts:118-132` pins zone containment ("keeps focus inside the grid rather than escaping to another zone") with 14 generic buttons. It does not pin the absence of a month step.
  - Not deliberate.
- **reach:** `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/calendar.tsx:11`, `:34`, `:39`. Apps: NONE, because DatePicker steers `active` across months (`packages/ui/src/components/date-picker/use-date-picker-state.ts:99-106`).
- **severity:** low. Only the docs reach it, and the chevrons still change the month.

###### C1-C11
- **verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - A seeded month is in the server markup. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-month.ts:100` `const shown = hydrated || value != null || defaultValue != null`
  - Each side reads the seed in its own zone:
    - `:96` `useState(() => monthOf(value ?? defaultValue ?? new Date()))`
    - `:17` `return firstOfMonth(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth())`
    - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-grid.tsx:83` `const isSelected = value != null && isSameDay(date, value)` (local `dayNumber` at `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:69-70`)
  - `new Date('2025-07-01')` is 00:00 UTC. A UTC server draws July with 31 options. Los Angeles reads June 30, so it draws June with 30. That is a text and node mismatch, which reaches `onRecoverableError` (`packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-hydration.test.tsx:19-20`).
  - Mid-month, only attributes differ. `packages/ui/node_modules/react-dom/cjs/react-dom-client.development.js:5440` "A tree hydrated but some attributes of the server rendered HTML didn't match the client properties. This won't be patched up". The cells are memoized (`packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-day-cell.tsx:36` `export const CalendarDayCell = memo(function CalendarDayCell({`), so the server's mark stays.
- **trigger:** As stated.
- **intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-month.ts:77-79` "A clock-seeded view (no `value` and no `defaultValue`) reads the clock of the side that renders it. Across a timezone offset at a month boundary, the server and the client can read different months." The guard covers the clock only.
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/use-calendar-month.test.ts:225-229` makes server display of a seeded month deliberate.
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-hydration.test.tsx:91-95` uses a seed from local parts in one zone (`:9-13`). Nothing covers a seed instant whose day differs between zones.
  - Not deliberate.
- **reach:** NONE.
  - The server-rendered root calendars are `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/calendar.tsx:11` (state starts `useState<Date | null>(null)` at `:7`, so the view is clock-seeded and held back), `:34` (axis props), and `:39` (no seed).
  - The apps mount calendars only in the portaled DatePicker dialog.
- **severity:** low. No shipped surface server-renders a seed that varies by zone. When hit, the month-boundary case heals by client re-render, but the mid-month case keeps a wrong selected mark.
- **group:** G1
- **priorArt:** NONE (terms: hydration, time zone, seed, server component).
- **dependency:** react-dom 19.2.7 (`packages/ui/node_modules/react-dom/package.json:3`). The same surfaces as C03 apply: `pnpm-workspace.yaml:47-50` and `:64-65`, `tsconfig.base.json:19-20`, and no Vite/Vitest alias. None applies. The vendored-copy search was skipped for the same reason.

  - The docs `<html>` has `lang="en"` and no `dir` (`packages/ui/src/docs/app/root.tsx:28`).
- **severity:** low. No shipped surface renders a range right-to-left, and the cost is cosmetic.

- **dependency:** none.

##### §4.2 Scope sheet

**Header:** Scope C1, Calendar: `packages/ui` C components, audit 2026-10-04.

###### Groups
- **G1 (C05, C11):** The server render reads runtime state the browser does not share: the runtime default locale (C05), and the runtime zone's day of a seeded instant (C11). The hydration guard holds back only clock reads (`packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-month.ts:100`, `useCalendarToday`). Prior art: NONE (terms: hydration, locale, time zone, prerender).
- **G2 (C08, C09):** A standalone calendar hands its grid keys to `useA11yRoving`'s index grid. That grid wraps at the list ends and has no Page keys. The date model exists only when a parent steers `active`. Prior art: NONE (terms: keyboard, roving, Page, month edge).
- **Independent:** C01, C02, C03, C06, C07, C13, C14.

###### Ordered steps

**S1: closes C05, C11 (G1)**
- **Change:** Apply the server-markup contract that Q1 settles to the locale and to the day of a seeded instant. Add hydration cases for a runtime-locale difference and a seed that varies by zone.
- **Files:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx`
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-month.ts`
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-grid.tsx`, if the selection waits for hydration
  - `packages/ui/src/utilities/resolve-locale.ts`, if the fallback changes
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-hydration.test.tsx`
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/use-calendar-month.test.ts`
- **Order:** 1. Highest severity with live reach (C05 on the docs). S5 edits the same files and the same `shown` rule.
- **Gate:** Q1.
- **§10.3:** No barred lifecycle sits under it. renderToString plus hydrateRoot (`calendar-hydration.test.tsx:22-48`) is the seam.
  - C11 needs a TZ change between the two renders, in the fork project (`packages/ui/vitest.config.ts:283-285`).
  - C05's locale is read once per process (`resolve-locale.ts:11` `once`), so a case needs a mocked `resolveLocale` or a fresh module.

**S2: closes C07**
- **Change:** Seat one Tab stop in each composite:
  - the day listbox: the selected day, else today, else the first enabled day
  - the picker listbox: `[data-selected]`
  - the header toolbars, as Q6 settles

  Do this through `manageTabIndex` and `activeSelector` on the `useA11yRoving` calls in `useCalendarFocus`.
- **Files:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-focus.ts`
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-picker.tsx`
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-toolbar.tsx`, only if Q6 drops the role
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/use-calendar-focus.test.ts`
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar.test.tsx`
- **Order:** 2. Next severity with live reach. S3 changes the same handler, and its month step must keep the stop.
- **Gate:** Q6.
- **§10.3:** The picker listbox sits in a floating-ui popover. Seams: `useCalendarFocus` on a DOM fixture (`use-calendar-focus.test.ts:6-20`) and `useCalendarPicker` (`use-calendar-picker.test.ts:9-11`).

**S3: closes C08, C09 (G2)**
- **Change:** Give a calendar that no parent steers the keyboard date model Q2 settles: a month step when an arrow leaves the month, and Page or Shift+Page for month or year, through `useCalendarMonth`'s steppers.
- **Files:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-focus.ts`
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx`
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar.test.tsx` (keyboard section, `:480-612`)
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/use-calendar-focus.test.ts`
- **Order:** 3.
- **Depends on:** S2 (same handler).
- **Gate:** Q2.
- **§10.3:** None barred; jsdom.

**S4: closes C13**
- **Change:**
  - Format the picker years (the month-view center label, the decade label, the year cells) in the calendar locale with no grouping separator.
  - Thread `localeTag` from Calendar through CalendarHeader and CalendarPicker into `useCalendarPicker`.
  - `formatInteger` is the wrong tool here: its `integer` spec sets only fraction digits (`packages/ui/src/utilities/format.ts:70-75`), so grouping stays on.
- **Files:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-picker.tsx`
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-picker.tsx`
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-header.tsx`
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx`
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/use-calendar-picker.test.ts` (`:106` and `:112` assert number labels)
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar.test.tsx`
- **Order:** 4. Not gated. It follows S1 and S2 because they share files.

###### Open questions

**Q1 (gates S1): What may the server markup depend on?**
- **Axes:**
  - (a) Props only. The default locale and a seed's zone-dependent day wait for hydration, as `today` and the clock-seeded month already do.
  - (b) The environment stays readable on the server, and agreement becomes the consumer's duty: an explicit `locale` or LocaleProvider, and seeds built from local parts.
  - (c) A locale fallback that both sides read the same. This answers C05 only.
- **What each changes:**
  - (a) drops the weekday row, the month label, and seeded days from server markup unless a locale is set. It changes `calendar-hydration.test.tsx:65-75` and `:91-108`, and `use-calendar-month.test.ts:225-229`.
  - (b) leaves the calendar code as is, and S1 adds cases that pin the limit.
  - (c) edits `resolve-locale.ts` or `calendar.tsx` for C05 and leaves C11 to (a) or (b).

**Q2 (gates S3): Does a calendar no parent steers carry the date-grid keyboard model, or does that stay with DatePicker?**
- **Carry it:** S3 adds the date-aware path. Home and End stay at the month ends that `calendar.test.tsx:520` pins (C10 is ruled out).
- **DatePicker only:** S3 changes no code.

**Q3 (gates S9): Is `hoverDate` with no `rangeStart` a supported CalendarRange state?**
- **Supported:** S9 edits `computeRangeDayFlags`.
- **Parent's duty:** The gate at `use-date-picker-range-state.ts:264` is the contract, and S9 changes no code.
- **Both:** The selected hover end with a start stays (`calendar-range.test.tsx:90-93`).

**Q4 (gates S5): Does the view seed follow the value cascade?**
- **(a)** `defaultValue` seeds only an uncontrolled, unbound calendar. A controlled-null or bound calendar becomes clock-seeded, and its month waits for hydration.
- **(b)** `defaultValue` seeds the view in every mode, and S5 changes no code.

**Q5 (gates S6): Which zone defines "local midnight" in `fromCalendarDate`?**
- **The runtime zone** that every other read uses (`calendar-utilities.ts:27-28`): S6 edits one function, and an app override no longer reaches the calendar.
- **The library zone:** every Date read moves to it (`toCalendarDate`, `dayNumber`, `monthOf`, `stepMonth`, the Intl formatters, `useCalendarToday`), across three files.

**Q6 (gates S2): Is each `role="toolbar"` row (the month header and the picker header) one composite with one Tab stop, or plain buttons?**
- **Keep the role:** S2 seats a stop in each toolbar.
- **Drop the role:** S2 edits `calendar-toolbar.tsx` and seats stops only in the two listboxes.
- **Both:** Each listbox gets one stop.

### C1: overturn report

Overturn pass, scope C1 (Calendar). Twelve verdicts stand. One record, C03, needs a correction but keeps its verdict: the sheet ruled out the sweep's OS-zone route with a guard that does not hold when the library's cached zone is `UTC`. All prior-art matches stay NONE. No group changes.

Path prefixes are the ones the sheet uses (`CAL/`, `UI/`, `T/`, `DEMOS/`, `DOCS/`, `APPS/`, `NM/`, `ROOT/`), plus two more:
- `PDOCS/` = packages/ui/docs/
- `AUD/` = packages/ui/docs/audits/

##### 1. Claims

**C1-C01: STANDS.** The parent drops a hover that comes before the first endpoint: `packages/ui/src/components/date-picker/date-picker-range-reducer.ts:52` "A hover before the first endpoint shows no band, and a hover on the" and `:55` `if (state.rangeStart === null || sameHoverDate(state.hoverDate, action.date)) return state`. That supports reach NONE and the parent's-duty arm of Q3, but nothing in CalendarRange makes the case deliberate. `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-range.test.tsx:108` covers a lone `rangeEnd` for the month view only.

**C1-C02: STANDS.**
- Two sources state that the skeleton matches the calendar: `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/calendar.ts:9-10` "…so the silhouette takes the step of its nearest density / scope, as the calendar does.", and `packages/ui/docs/COMPONENTS.md:80` "It has the box of the real component…".
- `CONVENTIONS.md:43` "It reads no context" limits how the fix can work. It does not make the gap deliberate.
- `packages/ui/src/__tests__/helpers/size-axes.ts:21` `CalendarSkeleton: ['sm', 'md', 'lg'],` covers no xs scope.
- The sweep's ReadyReveal and root-xs routes stay ruled out: `packages/ui/src/primitives/ready-reveal/ready-reveal.tsx:111-112`, and `packages/ui/src/core/density/steps.ts:5-7`.

**C1-C03: CORRECTED. The verdict stays NARROWED, but the dropped OS-zone part is too wide.**
- **Overturning source:** the guard that the ruled-out entry quotes (`conversion.mjs:103`) never runs when the cached zone is `UTC`.
  - `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/dist/private/conversion.mjs:99` `if (timeZone === 'UTC') return $d07e34cce18680fd$export$bd4fb2bc8bb06fb(dateTime);` returns first.
  - That function builds the instant in UTC: `:31-32` `date.setUTCHours(hour, minute, second, millisecond);` / `date.setUTCFullYear(year, month - 1, day);`.
  - The zone it tests is the first read, kept for the life of the process: `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/dist/private/queries.mjs:92` `if ($ad063034c8620db8$var$localTimeZone == null) $ad063034c8620db8$var$localTimeZone = new Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone;`.
  - The suite states the same split for Node: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/modules/chart-time.test.ts:306` `// Node reads a new TZ at once, and the date library keeps its own zone.`
- **trigger (verified):**
  - (1) As in the sheet: an app calls `setLocalTimeZone` with a zone east of the runtime zone, then renders `<Calendar />`.
  - (2) Restored in part: the runtime zone is exactly `UTC` when the library first reads it, and the OS zone then moves to a zone west of UTC while the tab stays open. Each later `fromCalendarDate` returns UTC midnight, which is the evening before in the new zone, and every bullet of the C03 trace follows.
  - From any other first zone, `:103` takes the native path, and the sheet's drop holds.
- **reach:**
  - (1) NONE, as in the sheet.
  - (2) No consumer builds this trigger; the shipped default reaches it. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:46` `return date.toDate(getLocalTimeZone())` runs in every calendar:
    - standalone: `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/calendar.tsx:11`, `:34`, `:39`;
    - through DatePicker: `apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-form-drawer.tsx:246` `<DatePicker name="visitedAt" />`, `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/date-picker.tsx:14`, `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/filters.tsx:136`, `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/providers/glass.tsx:145`, `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/modules/grid/editable.tsx:127`.
  - Not verified in this pass: that a browser moves `Date` to a new OS zone in an open tab.
- **severity:** low. The level is unchanged; the reason is new. Every calendar can reach (2), but only in a session whose first zone read was UTC and whose zone then moves west, and a reload reads the zone again. Until that reload, every label and both chevrons are wrong.
- **intent, group, priorArt:** unchanged. CHART D7 (`packages/ui/docs/audits/2026-09-28-CHART-AUDIT.md:23`, "Fixed in #1637") is a `Date.parse` seam in the chart, not a match.
- **dependency:** unchanged, re-read:
  - `pnpm-workspace.yaml:47-50`: overrides cover esbuild, postcss, and undici only.
  - `:64-65`: patchedDependencies lists `'@floating-ui/react@0.27.19'` only, and `patches/` holds only that patch.
  - `tsconfig.base.json:19-20`: `@/*` only.
  - The `ui` Vite and Vitest configs have no `alias` match. They name the package only at `vitest.browser.config.ts:217` and `packages/ui/src/docs/engine/vite/index.ts:107`.
  - The vendored-copy search the sheet skipped: no match in `packages/ui/src`, with `__benchmarks__` excluded.
  - None applies.

**C1-C04: REFUTED, and the refutation stands.** The sweep cites REFERENCE §2 (`packages/ui/REFERENCE.md:31` "a JS value takes the stored step one render after hydration"). That route needs a root at xs, and the root never takes xs: `packages/ui/src/core/density/steps.ts:5-7` "…the root element and `DensityProvider`, takes one of them. `xs` and `xl` are the outer steps. - I broke the search-scope rule in a few places, and I want you to know. To trace guards, I ran Grep or Glob in dependency folders that are outside the scope list and the roots: UI/components/{form,fieldset,control,drawer,address-input}, `packages/ui/src/primitives/virtual-options`, `packages/ui/src/utilities`, `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/select.ts`, `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso`, and `packages/ui/src/docs/engine`. None of them is an audit or benchmark path. Everything else I traced, I read by direct path.
- Wrappers of Combobox inside `packages/ui/src/components` were NOT searched for, because of the scope rule. I found AddressInput because a comment at `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox.tsx:590-591` points to it ("Async option swaps for an unchanged query (e.g. address suggestions"). I then read it and searched the roots for its call sites. I also listed the apps' "from 'ui/…'" imports: no other module they import wraps a Combobox (I read `packages/ui/src/components/filters/index.ts` to check Filters).
- Reach facts that several records share:
  - apps/admin, apps/places, `packages/ui/src/modules` and `packages/ui/src/layouts` render no Combobox directly.
  - apps/places renders one through AddressInput: `apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-search-field.tsx:54` "&lt;AddressInput" → `packages/ui/src/components/address-input/address-input.tsx:177` "&lt;Combobox&lt;AddressSuggestion>". That field sits inside a Drawer (`apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-form-drawer.tsx:133` "&lt;Drawer") and a Form (`apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-form-drawer.tsx:157` "&lt;Form&lt;PlaceValues>").
  - AddressInput passes these to the Combobox (address-input.tsx:190-195): "clearOnEmpty", "clearable={clearable}", "suffix={suffix}", "open={ready && menuRequested}", "onOpenChange={setMenuRequested}", "onQueryChange={setQuery}".
  - Docs roots: `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/combobox.tsx` (lines 44, 63, 91, 146, 174), `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/providers/glass.tsx:134`, `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/primitives/skeleton.tsx:127`, and `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/address-input.tsx` (lines 83, 101, 136, 160, 200, through AddressInput).
  - Both apps compile ui with the React Compiler: apps/places/next.config.ts:17 "reactCompiler: true," and apps/admin/next.config.ts:17 "reactCompiler: true,".
- Dependency surfaces: I cite no line under node_modules.
  - pnpm-workspace.yaml:64-65 patches only "'@floating-ui/react@0.27.19': patches/@floating-ui\_\_react@0.27.19.patch". That patch is the portal-node fix, and no record relies on a floating-ui line.
  - React/react-dom resolve to "^19.2.7" (pnpm-workspace.yaml:25-26), with no override and no patch.
  - Where a verdict depends on a runtime fact (React 19 discrete-event flush, the HTML microtask checkpoint, the input value setter, IME key values, paste on a read-only input, useDeferredValue timing), the record says "Premise". That premise was not checked against a node_modules line.
- Prior-art digest: I searched it for Combobox, Escape, focus, highlight, virtual, nullable, required, autoComplete, capitalize, summarize, title, paste, fieldset, onOpenChange, onQueryChange, address-input, IME, touch, keyboard and scroll. The only hits are:
  - DOCS-SITE-AUDIT §9 ruled-out: "Every `displayValue={(v) => v}` on Select and Combobox is required." This is about a demo prop, not any seam here.
  - D49, "| D49 | components/address-input.tsx:98, 176, 185; context-menu.tsx:9-21 | A copy of mockGooglePlaces inline; …". It names the address-input demo file but records a different problem.
  - IOS-TOUCH S2–S4, which are about touch-hold text selection on a context Menu.
  So priorArt is NONE for every claim and every group.
- Severity scale: high / medium / low.

C2-C01 — CONFIRMED
- mechanism:

  - :62 "const name = deferredQuery.trim()"

  - The row's value is the deferred text. Between the urgent commit (the input shows "Berlin") and the deferred commit, the row holds "Berli", and Enter clicks it through the sole-option shortcut or the seeded highlight.
  - Premise: useDeferredValue commits the new value in a later, interruptible render, so a keydown can land between the two commits.

- intent:

The row therefore appears in the frame the matches update" / "in, and the highlight lands on it rather than trailing a frame behind." It explains when the row renders, not what value it commits.
- reach: NONE for the heavy-list trigger. I searched the roots for ComboboxCreateOption, useComboboxDeferredQuery and &lt;Combobox. The only create row is `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/combobox.tsx:101` "&lt;ComboboxCreateOption taken={options} />", after a six-name list (:97 "&lt;FilteredPeople options={options} />"). There the deferred pass is light.

C2-C19 — CONFIRMED
- mechanism:
  - The input closes on Escape without preventDefault: `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts:172-175` "if (event.key === 'Escape') {" / "close()" / "" / "return".
  - The combobox's layer exists only while open: `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-escape-layer.ts:59` "if (!open || !enabled || order === null) return" and :63 "const unregister = layered ? registerDismissLayer(layer, order) : undefined", with cleanup at :75-78 "return () => {" / "unsubscribe()" / "" / "unregister?.()".
  - :66-68 "if (event.key !== 'Escape' || event.defaultPrevented) return" / "" / "if (layered && !isTopDismissLayer(layer)) return"
  - `packages/ui/src/utilities/document-listener.ts:42` "for (const h of [...reg.handlers]) {"
  - The surface around it: `packages/ui/src/primitives/overlay/overlay.tsx:147-149` "useDismissable({" / "open," / "onDismiss: () => onOpenChange(false)," → `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-dismissable.ts:55` "useEscapeLayer({"; `packages/ui/src/components/drawer/drawer.tsx:272` "&lt;Overlay".
  - Order of events:
    1. React's root listener runs close().
    2. The browser runs microtasks before the document listener.
    3. React commits open = false and flushes the discrete render's passive effects, so the combobox's layer unregisters.
    4. The document listener now finds the Overlay's layer on top, and closes it.
  - Premise: React 19 flushes a discrete update in a microtask and runs its passive effects at commit, and browsers run microtasks between listeners of a trusted event. jsdom's synthetic dispatch skips that checkpoint, so the jsdom suite cannot show this.
- trigger: as stated (real browser).
- intent: `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-escape-layer.ts:28-31` "Escape-key dismissal routed through the shared dismiss-layer stack, so" / "stacked surfaces (menu in dialog, dialog over sheet) close one per press," / "innermost first. Presses a consumer already handled (`preventDefault`)" / "are ignored."
- reach: apps/places. `apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-form-drawer.tsx:133` "&lt;Drawer" → :205 "&lt;PlaceSearchField />" → `apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-search-field.tsx:54` "&lt;AddressInput" → address-input.tsx:177. The entry is lost because the form re-seeds on each open: `apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-form-drawer.tsx:158-160` "// The drawer unmounts its children while closed, so the form re-seeds" / "// from `defaultValues` on each open and an abandoned entry never comes" / "// back. …"
- severity: high. A single Escape on open address suggestions closes the add/edit place drawer and discards the entry, on a shipped app path.
- group: G1 | priorArt: NONE | dependency: none.

====================================================================
SCOPE SHEET (§4.2) — C2
====================================================================

GROUPS
- G1. Members: C2-C12, C2-C19.
  - Cause: Escape is handled in two places, the input's onKeyDown and the escape layer that useFloatingUI registers, and neither tells the other which press the combobox consumed.
  - priorArt: NONE.
- G2. Members: C2-C22, C2-C27.
  - Cause: the open and query setters report every call to the consumer, not only a change.
  - priorArt: NONE.
- G3. Members: C2-C08, C2-C09.
  - Cause: reanchorOnOptionSwap infers the highlight state from activeIndexRef (indexed) or from a dangling aria-activedescendant (DOM), and keeps no record of whether the highlight was seeded, moved by the user, or left empty by design.
  - priorArt: NONE (D49 only names the address-input demo).
- G4. Members: C2-C07, C2-C18.
  - Cause: the lock is a per-path guard that reads only the props and Control channel. So a channel it does not read (an ancestor fieldset with disabled) and a path it does not wrap (paste) can still commit.
  - priorArt: NONE.

ORDERED STEPS
Each step gives: rows closed; change; files; order and reason; gate; §10.3 seam.

S1 — closes C2-C19 and C2-C12 (G1)
- Change: in the input's Escape branch, call event.preventDefault() when the menu was open, so the layer stack sees the press as consumed. Make the escape layer skip a keydown that is part of an IME composition (the same test as `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts:52`).
- Files: `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts` and `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-escape-layer.ts`. The second is shared by every overlay.
- Order 1: the only high-severity row, on a shipped app path. No dependency.
- Gate: none.
- Seam: call useComboboxInput().onKeyDown with makeKeyEvent('Escape') and open true or false, and assert preventDefault. Mount useEscapeLayer with renderHook and dispatch a composing Escape on document. Both are synchronous; neither drives floating-ui.

S2 — closes C2-C20
- Change: in onKeyDown, run the sole-option Enter and the roving handler only while open. Keep the arrow-opens-a-closed-menu path.
- Files: `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts`.
- Order 2: medium severity on the places path. Comes after S1 because both edit the same handler.
- Gate: none.
- Seam: onKeyDown with open: false and an optionsRef that holds one option (the pattern at `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/use-combobox-input.test.ts:237-255`). Assert there is no click and no preventDefault.

S3 — closes C2-C08 and C2-C09 (G3)
- Change: keep the highlight's origin (empty by design, seeded, or moved by the user) in a ref beside activeIndexRef, and let reanchorOnOptionSwap act on it.
- Files: `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox.tsx`.
- Order 3: medium severity (C2-C09 on the places path).
- Gate: Q1.
- Seam: export reanchorOnOptionSwap as @internal and unit-test it with a DOM container, an input ref, and a plain-object source. No virtualizer is needed.

S4 — closes C2-C04
- Change: prevent the default of a mousedown on the panel chrome outside an option row, so DOM focus stays on the input. Keep a scrollbar press working; the floating hooks already detect one (`packages/ui/src/hooks/use-floating-ui.ts:482`).
- Files: `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox-panel.tsx`.
- Order 4: medium severity, shipped default. It comes before S5, because it removes the focus-in-panel path that causes the missed touched mark in C2-C11.
- Gate: none.
- Seam: render ComboboxPanel directly (as `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/combobox.test.tsx:932-953` does) and assert that a mouseDown on the "No results" output is default-prevented.

S5 — closes C2-C11
- Change: give useFloatingUI an onOpenChange that closes through close() (and keeps the lock for an open), so an outside press resets editing and the query.
- Files: `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox.tsx` (the adapter may live in `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-state.ts`).
- Order 5: depends on S4.
- Gate: none.
- Seam: test the adapter as a pure callback under renderHook. §10.3 bars driving floating-ui's outside press.

S6 — closes C2-C07 and C2-C18 (G4)
- Change: let the lock read what Q6 decides at event time (in the open guard, the select guard, and the suffix and frame handlers), and skip the consumer's onPaste while locked.

OPEN QUESTIONS
- Q1 (gates S3): what is the re-anchor policy?
  - (a) When rows mount after a filter change that seeded nothing, do we seed the top match on a hover device? Yes closes C2-C09 paths (a) and (b).
  - (b) Does a seeded highlight follow a new top match that mounts above it? "Follow" closes C2-C09 path (c); "stay" leaves it open.
  - (c) Does an empty highlight from a plain open stay empty when the window changes? Yes closes C2-C08.
- Q2 (gates S10): does Enter on an already-selected option turn off a nullable value?
  - Only the highlighted-row Enter does: S10 touches selectSoleOption only.
  - No Enter path does: S10 also touches the roving activation.
  - Both do: C2-C21 closes as deliberate.
- Q3 (gates S14): is a Combobox that mixes a VirtualOptions source with plain options supported?
  - Supported: the shared roving hook changes.
  - Unsupported: the change is Combobox-only (a warning, and the create row moves into the items).
- Q4 (gates S18): does an enclosing Control's autoComplete reach the Combobox input?
  - Control wins over the default: S18 runs.
  - The Combobox keeps 'off' unless its own prop is set: C2-C16 closes as deliberate, and the conflict between control.tsx:39-42 and combobox.tsx:120-121/:341-342 is yours to route.
- Q5 (gates S19): does capitalize cover the summarize output?
  - Only the displayValue output (the public TSDoc reading): S19 runs.
  - Every resolved display string (the internal TSDoc reading): C2-C03 closes as deliberate.
- Q6 (gates S6): what counts as disabled for the lock?
  - The input's native :disabled state at event time: this covers any ancestor fieldset, including the Form's lock during submit.
  - A context provided by the Form's Fieldset: S6 also touches `packages/ui/src/components/form` and `packages/ui/src/components/fieldset`.
  - Props and Control only: C2-C07 closes as deliberate.
- Q7 (gates S15): what does the create row commit while the deferred query lags?
  - The live trimmed query: the row's label can trail the committed value by one render.
  - Hold the commit or the sole-option Enter until the deferred query catches up: S15 gates the shortcut instead.

RULED-OUT LIST
- C2-C06 refuted. Guard: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/combobox-utilities.test.ts:305` "it('adds nothing with no resolver, since there are no labels to spell out', () => {".
- C2-C15 refuted. Guard: `packages/ui/src/components/address-input/address-input.tsx:94` "disabled. Otherwise it cedes the slot to the Combobox clear button." and :145-146 "… which the suffix below asks so an undefined" / "// suffix cedes the slot to the Combobox's own clear button."
- C2-C17 refuted. Guard: `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox.tsx:407-408` "// Derived per render: while no value is held on either channel, clicking the" / "// selected option clears it. …"
- C2-C24 refuted. Guard: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/use-combobox-input.test.ts:113` "it('does not clear in multi-select mode', () => {".
- C2-C03, dropped part "the default count string is rewritten". Guard: `packages/ui/src/utilities/capitalize-first.ts:12` "return value.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + value.slice(1)". The count starts with a digit, so nothing changes.
- C2-C11, dropped part "the menu does not hold its filter during the exit". Guard: `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-state.ts:181` "menuQuery: frozenQuery ? frozenQuery.value.query : query,". On this path the live query is never cleared, because close() does not run, so the filter is unchanged through the exit.
- C2-C21, refuted trigger "arrow-open, then Enter on the seated selection". Guards: `packages/ui/src/hooks/a11y/use-a11y-roving.ts:868` "Virtual mode: key (or keys) that clicks the active item. A menu passes" and `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox.tsx:133` "/** Clicking the selected option clears it. */". Enter is a documented click, and a click on the selected row is the documented nullable toggle.

SURFACED, NOT JUDGED (leads)
- L1 (cross-component seam, for a probe): AddressInput × Combobox highlight anchoring. AddressInput opens the panel only once results arrive (address-input.tsx:193 "open={ready && menuRequested}"). The query therefore changes while the panel is closed, `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox.tsx:559` "lastQueryRef.current = deferredQuery" catches up, and :583 treats the later open as a plain open. The first result set shows with no highlight, and Enter submits the places drawer form.
- L2 (adjacent defect, for a sweep): `packages/ui/src/hooks/a11y/use-a11y-roving.ts:358-362`. seedVirtualTopMatch seeds index 0 of a source without checking isDisabled. This is the filter-change twin of C2-C13.
- L3 (adjacent defect, for a sweep): `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox-panel.tsx:92` "if (event.key === 'Escape') onClose()" closes without preventDefault. With focus in the panel (C2-C04), the same press reaches an enclosing Overlay, as in C2-C19.
- L4 (adjacent defect, for a sweep): a seeded highlight is never scrolled into view (use-a11y-roving.ts:373, compare :605 "if (next) ctx.scrollWithin(next, { block: 'nearest' })"). Together with the open-time scroll to the selection, the top match can sit out of view even with the compiler on (a neighbor of C2-C05).
- L5 (cross-component seam, for a probe): isReservedTextboxKey is shared with the command palette (`packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts:47`). Does the palette's Escape path also close on a composing Escape (C2-C12's class)?
- L6 (cross-component seam, for a probe): `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-controllable.ts:78` reports every set for every control that uses the hook. This is C2-C22's cause outside the Combobox.
- L7 (cross-component seam, for a probe): an implicit submit from AddressInput's input (Enter with no highlight) inside the places Form can leave the panel open while form.tsx:124 disables the fieldset. Whether a click then commits depends on whether the browser blurs the newly disabled input. I did not verify this (C2-C07's class).
- L8 (adjacent, for a sweep): the late keyboardSettled open (C2-C23) sets AddressInput's menuRequested for a field that no longer has focus (address-input.tsx:194). I traced no visible effect.
- L9 (cross-component seam, for a probe): Listbox shares the selection snapshot of `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-deferred-toggle.ts:86`. A controlled open held true there would freeze the same way (C2-C26's class).

UNLOCATED
- None. Every claim's file and symbol was found.

### C2: overturn report

OVERTURN PASS: SCOPE C2 (Combobox), 2026-10-04 bug audit

**Result:** no verdict, severity, reach, group or prior-art match changes. Every CONFIRMED and NARROWED claim stands. No source makes one of them deliberate, and no open-audit row records one. The weighing of C2-C19 adds one open question, Q8. The sweep evidence names no guard, caller or consumer site that the sheet missed. Its two extra mechanisms are covered elsewhere: the second Escape handler of C2-C19 is already L3, and the bound-field sub-case of C2-C17 becomes L12.

**C2-C19 — STANDS.** I weighed it against IN:904 "- **caller runtime check:** Not reproduced: in Chromium, Escape closed only the panel".

The sheet's runtime premise has three parts. I checked two of them in the code that the build resolves:
- **The discrete update flushes in a microtask:** RD:18985 "scheduleMicrotask(function () {" → :18991 "processRootScheduleInMicrotask();" → :18853 "flushSyncWorkAcrossRoots_impl(syncTransitionLanes, !1);".
- **The passive effects of a sync commit flush at once:** RD:18323 "0 !== (pendingEffectsLanes & 3) && flushPendingEffects();". The Next build of places has the same lines (ND:19905 and ND:20582 "scheduleMicrotask(function () {").
- **Resolution surfaces (for RD):**
  - pnpm-workspace.yaml:25-26 "react: ^19.2.7" / "react-dom: ^19.2.7".
  - The overrides at :48-50 cover only esbuild, postcss and undici.
  - :64-65 patches only "'@floating-ui/react@0.27.19': patches/@floating-ui\_\_react@0.27.19.patch", which is the only file in `patches/`.
  - tsconfig.base.json:19-21 maps only "@/*".
  - The `ui` vitest/vite configs have no resolve.alias, and UI holds no vendored react-dom.
  - None of these applies to React. ND is Next's own compiled copy, and no repository entry applies to it.

The third part is still unverified: a microtask checkpoint between two listeners of one trusted event.
- The repository records the listener layout that the race needs: DOCS/plans/2026-08-26-PDF-VIEWER-HIGHLIGHTS-PLAN.md:333-336 "`useEscapeLayer` listens" / "on `document`, the node React's own root listener sits on under the App Router" … "`defaultPrevented` is the protocol that layer actually honours."
- Each other inner handler that takes Escape calls `preventDefault`: `packages/ui/src/components/date-picker/use-date-picker-keyboard.ts:100-101`, and `packages/ui/src/components/pdf-viewer/pdf-viewer-highlights.tsx:351-353` "`preventDefault` is" / "what says so: `useEscapeLayer` …". So the missing call is not deliberate.

The probe names no guard, and I found none. It can decide the claim only if its key was trusted, and the suite has two ways to press a key:
- `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/setup/act-environment.ts:4-5` "the floating-ui project drives" / "components with real CDP events (`vitest/browser` userEvent)" gives a trusted key.
- packages/ui/vitest.browser.config.ts:237 "'@testing-library/user-event'," gives a synthetic key, and a synthetic dispatch runs no checkpoint.
- Also, a Drawer held at a fixed `open` stays open whatever the trace says.
- IN does not say which of these the probe used.

So the trace stands and the severity stays high. Q8 asks for the probe that decides.

##### 2. Changes

- **Open questions:** add Q8. It follows from the weighing of C2-C19.
  - **Q8:** does C2-C19 reproduce with a trusted key? It bears on the severity of C2-C19 and on the place of S1 in the order. S1's change runs either way, because it follows the protocol at use-escape-layer.ts:30-31.
  - Decision axes: the key is a `userEvent` from `vitest/browser`, outside `act()`; focus is on the combobox input; the Drawer's `onOpenChange` is wired to state.

##### 3. Surfaced, not judged

- **L10 (cross-component seam, for a probe):** `packages/ui/src/components/menu/menu-content.tsx:192-193` "onKeyDown={(event) => {" / "if (event.key === 'Escape') close()" closes the menu with no `preventDefault`. A context menu keeps focus in its panel (:182 "autoFocus={!isDropdown}"). A context menu inside a Dialog is therefore in the class of C2-C19.
- **L11 (cross-component seam, for a probe):** `packages/ui/src/components/menu/use-menu-state.ts:175` "if (!open) clearVirtualActive(triggerRef)" relies on the premise that C2-C20 breaks (use-a11y-roving.ts:113). I did not check whether an Enter on the trigger during the exit clicks a stale `data-active` row.
- **L12 (adjacent defect, for a sweep):** `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox.tsx:410` and `packages/ui/src/components/listbox/listbox.tsx:200` read `defaultValue` to derive `nullable`. CONVENTIONS.md:123 says "A bound field ignores the control's `defaultValue`/`defaultChecked`". A bound field with a stray `defaultValue` therefore loses click-to-clear. This comes from the evidence of C2-C17.
- **L13 (adjacent defect, for a doc sweep):** `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox.tsx:75-76` and :92 promise a hover title for "every selection at all when there is no {@link ComboboxBaseProps.displayValue}". `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/combobox-utilities.test.ts:305-306` pins no title when there is no resolver (C2-C06).

##### Pass notes
- **Inputs.** Outside the repo I opened only the input file. I did not open `packages/ui/docs/audits/` or `packages/ui/src/__benchmarks__`, and I met no sweep evidence and no benchmark.

  - the C11 bidi order (UAX #9 by hand)

- **node_modules.** No record cites a `node_modules` line, so `dependency` reads "none cited" in every record. `tsconfig.base.json:19-21` maps only `@/*`. The React radio change semantics in C02 and C17 are described, not cited.

  - `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-picker-trigger.tsx:102` `<span className={cn(k.value, 'min-w-0 flex-1 font-mono')}>{label}</span>`. The span has no `dir` and no isolation.
  - UAX #9 in an RTL paragraph:
    - In "#FF0000", '#' (ET) is next to L. W6 makes it ON, and N2 resolves it to R, so the label shows "FF0000#".
    - In "#00FF00", '#' is next to EN. W5 makes it EN, all seven characters resolve to level 2, and the label shows "#00FF00".
  - So a leading letter or a leading digit decides where '#' goes.
- **trigger:** as stated.
- **intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-picker-trigger.tsx:38`
  - `packages/ui/REFERENCE.md:31`: Portal writes "the direction of the nearest direction scope as `dir`", so RTL is a carried mode.
  - The inconsistency is wrong under either answer to Q5.
- **reach:** NONE. Searched `rtl|dir=|direction|DirectionProvider` in all roots. The only hits are Flex `direction` props in the demos and `packages/ui/src/layouts/sidebar/sidebar.tsx:120` `const rtl = useIsRtl()`; no hit renders a picker in an RTL scope.
- **severity:** low. A reading glitch, and no RTL site.
- **group:** G4
- **priorArt:** NONE. Searched rtl, dir, bidi, direction.
- **dependency:** none cited.

##### §4.2 Scope sheet: C3 (Color: ColorPanel, ColorPicker, and their parts)

###### Groups
- **G1:** equalHsva compares rounded HSV channels and an unwrapped hue, not the rendered color. Members: C02, C07. priorArt: NONE (searched equalHsva, swatch, chip, render, Color).
- **G2:** ColorPicker rebuilds its HSVA from the panel's wire value (`color-picker.tsx:104`), and the panel's own HSVA dies on unmount. Members: C09, C04. priorArt: NONE (searched hue, reopen, round, serialize, ColorPicker).
- **G3:** useColorPickerState drops part of the cascade that it resolves: `setTouched` from useFormValue and `readOnly` from useControlProps. Members: C01, C15. priorArt: NONE for this seam. D37 is a different seam (CurrencyInput in the grid module).
- **G4:** the hex text takes the inherited direction. Members: C11, C12. priorArt: NONE (searched rtl, dir, bidi, direction).
- **Independent:** C03, C05, C06, C08, C10, C13, C14, C16, C17, C18.

###### Ordered steps
Below, `color/` means `packages/ui/src/components/color/` and `tests/` means `packages/ui/src/__tests__/`.

1. **S1 (C02, C07).**
   - **Change:** make equalHsva compare what renders, the RGB bytes and the alpha byte. Then h 0 equals h 360, and #fefefe differs from #ffffff.
   - **Files:** `color/color-utilities.ts`, `tests/components/color.test.tsx`.
   - **Order:** first. The swatch check and the echo guard read equalHsva, and S3 and S4 depend on its meaning.
   - **Gate:** none.
   - **§10.3 seam:** the pure function (`color.test.tsx:103-115` already drives it).
2. **S2 (C08).**
   - **Change:** pin `a` to 1 wherever useColorState adopts a color while `alpha` is off: the seed, the reconcile effect, and a switch of `alpha` to false.
   - **Files:** `color/use-color-state.ts`, `color.test.tsx`.
   - **Order:** independent; before S3, which edits the same file.
   - **Gate:** none.
   - **§10.3 seam:** `renderHook(useColorState)`.
3. **S3 (C03).**
   - **Change:** per Q2, set what the echo guard compares for an HSVA value.
   - **Files:** `color/color-utilities.ts`, `color/use-color-state.ts`, `color.test.tsx`.
   - **Order:** after S1 (meaning of equalHsva) and after S2 (shared file).
   - **Gate:** Q2.
   - **§10.3 seam:** `renderHook(useColorState)`, as `color.test.tsx:517-543` does.
4. **S4 (C09, and the confirmed part of C04).**
   - **Change:** share one HSVA between the picker and its inline panel. The panel seeds from the picker's HSVA on mount and writes the picker's HSVA on change, with no wire round trip. The picker serializes once, at its own edge. Keep the channel internal: a new public ColorPanel prop is a public export change (CLAUDE.md §3.1).
   - **Files:** `color/color-picker.tsx`, `color/color-panel.tsx`, `color/context.ts`, `color/use-color-picker-state.ts`, `color.test.tsx`.
   - **Order:** after S1, because the hsva echo through the picker uses equalHsva.
   - **Gate:** none.
   - **§10.3 seam:** render ColorPanel through the internal channel, plus `renderHook(useColorState)`. The reopen itself needs the Portal exit (motion) and floating-ui, which §10.3 bars.
5. **S5 (C05).**
   - **Change:** drop or re-seed a field's draft when the color changes from a source other than that field. The field then shows the live color, and a stale shorthand cannot commit on blur.
   - **Files:** `color/use-color-field.ts`, `color/color-hex-input.tsx`, `color/color-channel-inputs.tsx`, `color.test.tsx`.
   - **Order:** after S4, which changes the write path that the eyedropper and the swatches take in the picker.
   - **Gate:** none.
   - **§10.3 seam:** a controlled ColorPanel: `fireEvent.focus` on the hex field, a rerender with a new value, then `fireEvent.blur`. All synchronous.
6. **S6 (C01, C15).**
   - **Change:** wire the rest of the cascade into the picker: mark the bound field touched, and honor `readOnly`.
   - **Files:** `color/use-color-picker-state.ts`, `color/color-picker.tsx`, `color/color-picker-trigger.tsx`, `color.test.tsx`.
   - **Order:** after S4, which shares `color-picker.tsx`.
   - **Gates:** Q1 (C01) and Q4 (C15).
   - **§10.3 seam:** a Form-bound picker driven by the trigger's own toggle (`onOpenChange(!open)`) or by `fireEvent.blur`. Do not drive floating-ui's Escape or outside-press dismiss.
7. **S7 (C06).**
   - **Change:** paint the trigger swatch's edge above its fill.
   - **Files:** `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/color-picker.ts`, `color/color-picker-trigger.tsx`.
   - **Order:** after S6, which shares `color-picker-trigger.tsx`.
   - **Gate:** none.
   - **§10.3 seam:** the closed trigger involves no floating-ui. jsdom can hold the structure; only the browser suite sees the paint order.
8. **S8 (C10).**
   - **Change:** admit `null` in `value` on both arms of ColorValueProps and in ColorPickerStateOptions. This widens a public export, so CLAUDE.md §3.4 applies.
   - **Files:** `color/types.ts`, `color/use-color-picker-state.ts`.
   - **Order:** after S6, which shares `use-color-picker-state.ts`.
   - **Gate:** none.
   - **§10.3 seam:** `turbo run check-types`, plus a render with `value={null}`.
9. **S9 (C13).**
   - **Change:** focus without scrolling, or measure before the focus.
   - **Files:** `color/use-color-drag.ts`, `tests/components/use-color-drag.test.ts`.
   - **Order:** independent.
   - **Gate:** none.
   - **§10.3 seam:** the stub node at `use-color-drag.test.ts:12-24`, with a `focus` stub that moves the rect.
10. **S10 (C14).**
    - **Change:** make the area and the tracks honor a disabled ancestor fieldset: the press, the keys, and (per Q7) the tab stop.
    - **Files:** `color/use-color-drag.ts`, `color/color-area.tsx`, `color/color-slider.tsx`, and their tests.
    - **Order:** after S9, which shares `use-color-drag.ts`.
    - **Gate:** Q7, for the tab-stop part only.
    - **§10.3 seam:** render inside `<Fieldset disabled>`, then `fireEvent.pointerDown` and `fireEvent.keyDown`. Synchronous.
11. **S11 (C16).**
    - **Change:** write ColorPanelSkeleton by hand (§3.7), with the axes that move the silhouette: `alpha`, and the swatch rows or no swatches. Kokkaku holds the dimensions. This adds public props, so CLAUDE.md §3.4 applies.
    - **Files:** `color/color-panel-skeleton.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/color-panel.ts`, `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/dan/size.ts`, `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/color-panel.ts`, `tests/browser/geometry/skeleton-parity.test.tsx`.
    - **Order:** independent.
    - **Gate:** none.
    - **§10.3 seam:** the browser geometry parity test. No third-party lifecycle is involved.
12. **S12 (C17).**
    - **Change:** per Q6, set what `swatches` may hold.
    - **Files:** `color/color-swatches.tsx`, plus `color/color-utilities.ts` if parsing widens.
    - **Order:** after S3 if it touches `color-utilities.ts`.
    - **Gate:** Q6.
    - **§10.3 seam:** render with `swatches={['red']}` and a console spy.
13. **S13 (C18).**
    - **Change:** keep the swatch radios out of form submission.
    - **Files:** `color/color-swatches.tsx`, `color.test.tsx`.
    - **Order:** after S12, which shares the file.
    - **Gate:** none.
    - **§10.3 seam:** render inside a native form and read `new FormData(form)`. Synchronous.
14. **S14 (C11; C12 only under the LTR answer).**
    - **Change:** per Q5, render the label, and under LTR also the hex field, so the '#' sits in the same place for every color.
    - **Files:** `color/color-picker-trigger.tsx`, `color/color-hex-input.tsx`.
    - **Order:** after S5 and S7, which share these files.
    - **Gate:** Q5.
    - **§10.3 seam:** jsdom does no bidi layout. A structural check (a `dir` or an isolate on the text) is the only synchronous seam; the rendered order needs the browser suite.

###### Open questions
- **Q1 (S6, C01): when does the picker mark its field touched?**
  - On any panel close: setTouched rides `setOpen`, and the error appears on close.
  - On the trigger's blur, as FormValueResult says ("Call from `onBlur`"). The panel seats focus on its first tabbable (`color-picker-content.tsx:66` `trapFocusProps={{ initialFocus: 0 }}`), so this marks the field on open.
  - The answer decides the handler that carries setTouched and the moment the error shows.
- **Q2 (S3, C03): what does the echo guard compare for an HSVA value?**
  - Render-equality, as today: an owner that normalizes counts as an echo, and a controlled change in a hidden channel is dropped. C03 then stands as designed, and the decision refutes it.
  - Equality of the rounded channels: every controlled change is adopted, but an owner that normalizes 360 to 0 snaps the hue thumb on each emission. The hsva part of C07 comes back after S1.
- **Q4 (S6, C15): what does readOnly do to a picker, and where does it come from?**
  - Block the open (the DatePicker shape at `date-picker.tsx:400-401`), or open a panel with read-only parts.
  - The Control cascade only, or also a public `readOnly` prop on ColorPicker (a public export change under CLAUDE.md §3.1 and §3.4).
  - The answers decide whether the change touches the trigger only or the panel context and every part, and whether ColorPickerProps grows.
- **Q5 (S14, C11/C12): does a hex code read LTR inside an RTL scope?**
  - An LTR island: the label and the field read "#FF0000", and C12 is a defect.
  - Mirror with the Input affixes: the '#' sits at the inline start, and C12 is refuted. C11 still needs one placement of '#' for every color.
- **Q6 (S12, C17): what may `swatches` hold?**
  - Any CSS color: this needs a parser that also runs on the server, and the match then compares parsed bytes.
  - Hex only, rejected loudly: a development warning under `NODE_ENV`.
  - The answer moves the change into `color-utilities.ts` parsing or into a warning in `color-swatches.tsx`.
- **Q7 (S10, C14): must a disabled fieldset also take the area out of the tab order?**
  - Press and keys only: an event-time check of the element's `:disabled` ancestry, with no new state.
  - The tab stop too: this needs render-time knowledge of the ancestor's state, for example an observer or a native disabled element under the area.
  - The answer decides whether S10 stays in the handlers or adds state to `color-area.tsx`.

###### Ruled-out list
- **C02, the drag trigger at a full CSS pixel.** At every step, one pixel short of the top or bottom edge rounds the value away from 100 or 0:
  - `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-utilities.ts:31` `v: Math.round(clamp(v, 0, 100)),`
  - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/dan/size.ts:55` `area: 'density-h-[32,40,48]',`
  - At md (160px), one pixel gives v 99.375 (rounds to 99) at the top and v 0.625 (rounds to 1) at the bottom. sm gives 99.22 / 0.78; lg gives 99.48 / 0.52.
  - The white or black chip checks only within 0.5% of the area height: under 0.8px at md, which is one device pixel on a 2x display. The left-edge part holds at a full pixel (s 0.3 rounds to 0).
- **C04, the rounded `onValueChange` payload on its own,** and a standalone ColorPanel whose display is finer than its wire: both are deliberate per `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-utilities.ts:21-23` (the roundHsva TSDoc quoted in C04).

###### Surfaced, not judged
1. **Cross-component seam (for a probe).** An open ColorPicker panel portals out of the Form's disabled Fieldset (`packages/ui/src/components/form/form.tsx:124`). `packages/ui/src/components/color/use-color-picker-state.ts:70` resolves `disabled` only from the props and the Control, so the panel stays editable while a Form submits.
2. **Adjacent defect (for a sweep).** ColorPanel reads no Control cascade: `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-panel.tsx:63` takes `disabled` from its prop only, while ColorPicker reads the Control. So `<Control disabled>` leaves an inline panel enabled.
3. **Adjacent defect (for a sweep).** `packages/ui/src/components/control/control.tsx:41-42` lists the control-aware descendants without the color picker, which consumes the cascade.
4. **Adjacent defect (for a sweep).** `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-eyedropper.tsx:53-54` `} catch {` / `// The user dismissed the eyedropper (AbortError); nothing to commit.` swallows every rejection, not only AbortError.
5. **Adjacent defect (for a sweep).** `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-picker-content.tsx:77` keeps focus off every part that does not stop mousedown: the eyedropper, the copy button, and the swatch labels. A pointer press therefore leaves focus on the earlier control. This also feeds C05.
6. **Adjacent defect (for a sweep).** `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-swatches.tsx:59` `aria-label={swatch}` names each chip by its raw hex string.

###### UNLOCATED
None. I found every claim's symbol in its stated file.

### C3: overturn report

Overturn pass, scope C3. I opened only one file outside the repository: the input file. I did not open `packages/ui/src/__benchmarks__` and found no benchmark. For the C18 resolution check I grepped the `packages/ui` `vitest.bench*.config.ts` files for `alias` in count mode (0 hits); I read no other text from them. The sheet rules no claim REFUTED. The only caller check is C02's "Chromium" line, and it records no outcome, so it changes no verdict.

Path prefixes: `color/` = `packages/ui/src/components/color/`; `components/` = `packages/ui/src/components/`; `tests/` = `packages/ui/src/__tests__/`; `ui/` = `packages/ui/`.

**Prior art.** The four open audits in `packages/ui/docs/audits/` record no C3 seam. I searched for touched, setTouched, readOnly, fieldset, preventScroll, bidi, rtl, dir=, FormData, useId, 7.3, skeleton, silhouette, echo, draft, eyedropper, swatch, equalHsva, hsva and color. Every hit is another seam: DOCS-SITE A15, C4 (resolved), D37 and D45; CHART D26 and Q18 (fixed); CLEANUP X7 (fixed). Every priorArt NONE stands.
