##### 2. Changes that follow

- **Ruled-out list, add:**
  - C05: an outside write to a focused inline panel, and its blur commit. Guard: `packages/ui/src/components/color/use-color-field.ts:6-7` and `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-hex-input.tsx:19-20`.
  - C18: the ui `<Form>` arm. Guard: `use-form-reducer.ts:283` `event.preventDefault()`, with the React gate at `:19062` / `:19084-19086`.
- **S5 (C05), re-derived:**
  - **Change:** end the field edit when the eyedropper is pressed in the picker, so the documented blur commit runs before the pick and the draft clears. The eyedropper escapes the hold for its own press, as the field does at `color-hex-input.tsx:78` `onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}`. `use-color-field.ts` keeps its documented shadow.
  - **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-eyedropper.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/color.test.tsx`. The alternative is to limit the hold in `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-picker-content.tsx` to the area and the tracks; that also covers lead 5, which is not a row.
  - **Order:** independent. The old "after S4" reason no longer applies: S4 changes where the pick writes, not where focus sits.
  - **Gate:** none.
  - **§10.3 seam:** an inline ColorPanel inside a wrapper whose `onMouseDown` calls `preventDefault` (the hold, without floating-ui). Stub `window.EyeDropper` and restore it in `onTestFinished` (§10.9). Fire `fireEvent.mouseDown` on the eyedropper and assert that the wrapper's handler did not run. jsdom does not move focus on mousedown, so the focus outcome itself needs the browser suite.
- **S14:** order becomes "after S7, which shares `color-picker-trigger.tsx`". S5 no longer touches `color-hex-input.tsx`.
- **S13:** unchanged. It now closes C18's native-form arm.
- **S9:** `use-range-pointer.ts:77` is the in-library form of the "focus without scrolling" option.
- **Q1 (axes refined):**
  - Close arm: DatePicker already does this. `use-date-picker-state.ts:128-129` `// Closing the popover (select, clear, dismiss, or Escape) is the field's` / `// "blur" — mark it touched so validateOn="touched" rules can fire.`
  - Blur arm: it marks the field on open only if the blur is unguarded. Listbox guards it: `packages/ui/src/components/listbox/listbox.tsx:308-309` `// Marks the bound field touched when focus leaves the widget; a blur into the` / `// portaled panel (opening the menu) doesn't count. Mirrors the combobox`, and `:323` `if (refs.floating.current?.contains(next)) return`.
- **Q4 (axes refined):** DatePicker blocks every write, not only the open (`use-date-picker-state.ts:133` `// readOnly blocks every value write, not only the open paths. A controlled`, `:137`). It takes `readOnly` from both its prop and the Control (`:72`).
- **Q5:** the LTR arm has an in-library precedent: `code-block.tsx:214-215`, tested at `code-block.test.tsx:161`. CHART-AUDIT D26 (fixed) also pinned a physical bar with `dir="ltr"`.
- **Q7:** the tab-stop arm has an in-library pattern for tracking a fieldset at render time: `packages/ui/src/components/tooltip/use-tooltip-state.ts:52-53` ` * The `&lt;fieldset>` ancestors of `reference`, nearest first. A `disabled` change` / ` * on any of them changes whether the reference matches `:disabled`.`
- **Groups:** no change.

### C4 — CommandPalette, Confirm, and ContextMenu: blind verdict sheet

#### Scope C4: blind-pass verdict sheet (CommandPalette, Confirm, ContextMenu)

**Totals:** 23 CONFIRMED, 2 NARROWED (C4-C01, C4-C06), 1 REFUTED (C4-C07), 0 RESTATED, 0 UNLOCATED.

**What I opened and what I did not:**
- The only path under /tmp that I opened is the input file.
- I opened no file under `packages/ui/docs/audits/` or `packages/ui/src/__benchmarks__`.
- A benchmark path is named in a comment at `packages/ui/src/components/menu/menu-trigger.tsx:93`. I did not open it.
- I did not open the `vitest.bench*.config.ts` files.
- I scoped each search to the scope files, the consumer roots (including `__tests__`) or the intent sources. I read the dependencies by their import path.

**Prior-art digest.** I checked every claim against these entries:
- DOCS-SITE A15, D37, D42 and D49, plus its three §9 ruled-out entries.
- IOS S2, S3 and S4.
- CLEANUP has no covering entry. CHART is outside the area.

In the records, "NONE" means that no digest entry records the seam.

##### §4.1 Records

###### C4-C01: NARROWED
- **mechanism:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/command-palette/command-palette-item.tsx:83` `tabIndex: -1,` and `:124` `<button type="button" {...forwardedProps(props)} {...optionProps}>`. `optionProps` (`:80-89`) has no mousedown or pointerdown handler, so a press moves DOM focus to the option. Chromium and Firefox focus a pressed `tabindex=-1` button. I did not verify WebKit.
  - A disabled link renders `:97` `<span` with `:101` `tabIndex={-1}`, which also takes focus on a press.
  - `packages/ui/src/components/command-palette/command-palette.tsx:180` `onKeyDown={onKeyDown}` sits on the Input only. With focus on the option, typed keys miss the query and the arrow keys never reach roving.
  - For `closeOnAction={false}`, item `:74` `if (closeOnAction) close()` leaves the palette open. Enter or Space then clicks the focused row, which runs `:72` `onAction?.()` again.
  - The `:focus` wash comes from `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/hannou/tint.ts:13` `'not-disabled:not-data-disabled:hover:bg-zinc-950/5 not-disabled:not-data-disabled:focus:bg-zinc-950/5'`. It paints beside `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/hannou/active.ts:12` `data-active:bg-zinc-950/5`.
- **trigger:** Verified in full for `closeOnAction={false}` rows. For disabled rows, verified in part: the focus move, the lost typing, the lost arrow keys and the lost `aria-activedescendant` context. The same focus move also follows a press on any row that does not complete a click (press, drag off, release).
- **intent:**
  - command-palette.tsx`:91-92` "Arrow keys drive a virtual roving highlight via `aria-activedescendant` while focus stays on the input."
  - `packages/ui/src/components/command-palette/use-command-palette-state.ts:31-33` "the refs and `onKeyDown` that drive `aria-activedescendant` highlighting over options while focus stays on the input."
  - No test pins focus after a press.
- **reach:**
  - Stated trigger, test-only: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/command-palette.test.tsx:438`, `:460`, `:492`, `:510`.
  - No shipped site sets `closeOnAction` or `disabled`. I searched both terms on CommandPaletteItem in apps, demos, modules and layouts.
  - The press-without-click path reaches every row: `apps/places/src/components/place-palette/place-palette.tsx:62` and `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/command-palette.tsx:127`, `:167`.
- **severity:** low. No shipped site uses the stated trigger, the broader path is uncommon, and a click back into the input heals it.
- **group:** independent. **priorArt:** NONE (terms: palette, focus, option, closeOnAction). **dependency:** none.

###### C4-C02: CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - `handleSelect` has only the disabled guard. command-palette-item.tsx`:63` `if (disabled) {`, then `:70` `onClick?.(event)`, `:72` `onAction?.()`, `:74` `if (closeOnAction) close()`.
  - The closed palette stays mounted:
    - `packages/ui/src/primitives/portal/portal.tsx:25` "The open surface, mounted while `open` and kept through its exit animation."
    - portal.tsx`:117-118` `<AnimatePresence onExitComplete={handleExitComplete}>` / `{open && children}`.
  - The exit lasts 150 ms: `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/ugoki/popover.ts:14` `transition: { duration: duration[150], ease: ease.out },`.
  - The panel keeps `'pointer-events-auto',` (`packages/ui/src/components/dialog/dialog.tsx:183`).
  - Overlay renders its panel with no close-time guard (`packages/ui/src/primitives/overlay/overlay.tsx:167-196`). Compare `packages/ui/src/primitives/floating-surface/floating-surface.tsx:106` `node.style.pointerEvents = open ? (style?.pointerEvents ?? '') : 'none'`.
  - The exiting tree runs the handlers it had when last open. floating-surface.tsx`:97-99` "`AnimatePresence` holds the exiting subtree at the props it had when it was last open, so a prop keyed on `open` never reaches it."
  - The page click that is swallowed lands on the Overlay backdrop in the same exiting tree: overlay.tsx`:179` `{(backdrop || catchesPress) && (` … `:190` `onClick={dismissOnBackdrop ? () => onOpenChange(false) : undefined}`.
- **trigger:** As stated: a second press within the 150 ms exit. The page-click part sits in Overlay, not in CommandPaletteItem (lead L1).
- **intent:**
  - command-palette-item.tsx`:33-35` "Runs the consumer `onClick` then `onAction`, closing the palette afterward unless `closeOnAction` is false".
  - floating-surface.tsx`:91-95` "A closed surface is still on screen through its exit animation … it must stop taking input the moment it stops being real, or it swallows the hovers and presses meant for what lies under it."
- **reach:**
  - place-palette.tsx`:62-66` (`onAction={command.run}`, closes by default). The run callbacks are state sets plus one visit mutation (`apps/places/src/components/place-palette/place-palette-sources.tsx:183` `run: () => onMark(!marked),`).
  - Demo rows at `:127` and `:167` have no `onAction`.
- **severity:** low. The shipped actions are idempotent state sets or send one duplicate idempotent mutation.
- **group:** G1. **priorArt:** NONE. **dependency:** none.

###### C4-C03: CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - command-palette-item.tsx`:124` `<button type="button" {...forwardedProps(props)} {...optionProps}>`. `type` comes before the consumer spread.

- **trigger:** As stated.
- **intent:**
  - `CONVENTIONS.md:49` "The load-bearing structural attributes come after `{...props}`: `role`, `tabIndex`, `type`, … Then a stray prop cannot … turn a button into a form submit".
  - The sibling `packages/ui/src/components/command-palette/command-palette-close.tsx:42-43` writes `{...props}` and then `type="button"`.
- **reach:** NONE. I searched `type=` and prop spreads on CommandPaletteItem in apps, demos, modules and layouts.
  - place-palette.tsx`:62-66` passes `key`, `id` and `onAction`.
  - The demo `:167` spreads `meta`, which holds only `'aria-setsize'` and `'aria-posinset'` (`packages/ui/src/primitives/virtual-options/virtual-options.tsx:51-54`).
- **severity:** low. No site passes `type`.
- **group:** independent. **priorArt:** NONE. **dependency:** none.

###### C4-C04: CONFIRMED

###### C4-C05: CONFIRMED

The custom filter replaces the default at `:54` `return event.repeat || event.isComposing || target !== event.currentTarget && target.matches("[contenteditable],input,select,textarea");`.

###### C4-C09: CONFIRMED

###### C4-C12: CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - state `:112` `if (isReservedTextboxKey(event)) return` returns without `preventDefault`.
  - The close comes from the Dialog's escape layer: overlay.tsx`:147-149` `useDismissable({` … `onDismiss: () => onOpenChange(false),` → `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-dismissable.ts:55` `useEscapeLayer({` → `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-escape-layer.ts:66` `if (event.key !== 'Escape' || event.defaultPrevented) return`. There is no composition check. `:70` `dismiss(event)`.
  - The listener is a document bubble listener (`:73`; `packages/ui/src/utilities/document-listener.ts:56` `document.addEventListener(type, reg.listener)`), so it runs after the input's handler.
  - The close then resets the query (state `:162` `if (!open) setQuery('')`).
  - So the defect sits in the escape layer, not in the palette's roving.
- **trigger:** As stated. Whether a browser reports a composing Escape as `key: 'Escape'` cannot be checked in the repository. `packages/ui/src/utilities/is-composing.ts:11-12` notes that Safari reports 229 with `isComposing` false on a composition key.
- **intent:** `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts:45-47` "Every key of an IME composition also belongs to the textbox: the IME uses Enter to confirm the composition, Escape to cancel it, … Shared with the command palette's search textbox."
- **reach:** Shipped default, so every palette input. Sites: place-palette.tsx`:138`, demos.
- **severity:** low. Only IME users on browsers that report the key are hit, and the cost is a reopen and retype.
- **group:** independent. **priorArt:** NONE. **dependency:** none.

###### C4-C18: CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - confirm.tsx`:155` `onClick={onConfirm}` has no guard.
  - The closed dialog stays live for 150 ms: portal.tsx`:25`, `:117-118`; popover.ts`:14`; dialog.tsx`:183`. Overlay has no exit guard (compare floating-surface.tsx`:106`).
  - The exiting tree keeps the `onConfirm` it had when last open (floating-surface.tsx`:97-99`). A caller's own guard, such as users/client.tsx`:111` `if (deactivating) setActive({ userId: deactivating.id, isActive: false })`, therefore still sees the target from the open render.
  - Keyboard path: focus is returned only at unmount, in the cleanup of `packages/ui/node_modules/@floating-ui/react/dist/floating-ui.react.mjs:2034` `useModernLayoutEffect(() => {` (`:2086` `return () => {`). Its dependencies at `:2111` (`[disabled, floating, floatingFocusElement, returnFocusRef, dataRef, events, tree, isInsidePortal, domReference, getNodeId]`) hold no `open`. Focus therefore stays on the confirm button, and a quick second Enter clicks it.
- **trigger:** As stated.
- **intent:**
  - confirm.tsx`:95-96` "`onConfirm` leaves the dialog open so the caller decides when to dismiss."
  - floating-surface.tsx`:91-95`.
- **reach:**
  - users/client.tsx`:110-114`
  - security/client.tsx`:154-158`
  - connected-accounts.tsx`:129-133`
  - account/client.tsx`:167-171`
  - two-step.tsx`:196-200`
  - your-data.tsx`:81-85`: `remove.mutate()`, which deletes the account
  - places-app.tsx`:240-244`
  - demo confirm.tsx`:25`, `:42-45`
- **severity:** medium. A fast double press makes every app Confirm send its destructive mutation again, and the consumers' own guards do not stop it.
- **group:** G1.
- **priorArt:** NONE. D42's "Confirm toggles from the closure" is the demo checkbox at demos confirm.tsx`:65`, a different seam.
- **dependency:** @floating-ui/react 0.27.19 (`packages/ui/node_modules/@floating-ui/react/package.json:3`; import entry `:17` `"default": "./dist/floating-ui.react.mjs"`).
  - pnpm-workspace.yaml`:65` patches it with `patches/@floating-ui__react@0.27.19.patch`. The patch's hunks for this file touch only `useFloatingPortalNode` (`patches/@floating-ui__react@0.27.19.patch:99` `@@ -1532,12 +1532,12 @@ function useFloatingPortalNode(props) {`, `:117`, `:141`), not FloatingFocusManager.
  - No override applies.
  - tsconfig paths hold only `@/*`.
  - vitest.browser.config.ts lists it under `optimizeDeps.include` (`:212`) and mocks it per test instance only. No alias.
  - No vendored copy exists (overlay.tsx`:3`).

###### C4-C19: CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - `ConfirmAction` (confirm.tsx`:43-53`) holds `label`, `color` and `disabled` only.
  - `:154` `disabled={confirm?.disabled}` disables the button natively.
  - Button's pending state, which keeps focus, is never forwarded (button.tsx`:54` `loading = false,`; `:146` `{...(loading && loadingProps)}`).
  - Overlay passes no `restoreFocus` to its focus manager (overlay.tsx`:243-250`).

  - button.tsx`:36-38` "A loading button stays enabled, so it keeps the focus that it has. It is `aria-disabled`, leaves the tab order, and cancels each activation".
  - confirm.tsx`:91-92` "…whose labels, colors, and disabled state are configurable."

- **severity:** low.
- **group:** independent. **priorArt:** NONE. **dependency:** none.

  - `returnFocusTo` is `triggerRef` (`packages/ui/src/hooks/use-floating-disclosure.ts:124`). Only MenuTrigger attaches it (menu-trigger.tsx`:94-99`).

  - No focus manager restores either (menu-content.tsx`:157-163` has no `trapFocusContext`). Focus falls to `<body>` when the panel unmounts.
- **trigger:** As stated. Both the keyboard path and the pointer path with a focused field were verified.
- **intent:**
  - use-floating-ui.ts`:329-332` "Every other close (Escape, selection, programmatic) restores it."
  - menu-content.tsx`:180-181`.
  - The grid's bespoke menu restores only for a keyboard open (`packages/ui/src/modules/grid/grid-context-menu.tsx:172-173`); see Q11.
- **reach:** The chart module's shipped default wraps every chart (chart-context-menu.tsx`:441`; `packages/ui/src/modules/chart/engine/chart-frame/frame.tsx:679`), and the plot is focusable (chart-context-menu.tsx`:218`). Chart demos: `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/modules/chart/index.tsx:180`, `:203`. No app renders a chart.
- **severity:** medium. Every chart's keyboard menu path loses focus to the document start.
- **group:** independent. **priorArt:** NONE (IOS S2–S4 are about touch selection). **dependency:** none.

###### C4-C25: CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:** While inactive, context-menu.tsx`:60` drops `className`. While active, it lands on the contents root: menu.tsx`:116` `const Root = state.isDropdown ? 'span' : 'div'` and `:135` `className={cn('contents', isContextMenu && '[-webkit-touch-callout:none]', className)}`.
- **trigger:** As stated.
- **intent:** CONVENTIONS.md`:49` "Presentational attributes stay open to override, and `className` merges through `cn`." menu.tsx`:130-132` says the contents wrapper takes no layout. ContextMenuProps `className` (context-menu.tsx`:27`) has no TSDoc.
- **reach:** NONE. The chart (`:441-450`) passes no class, and the demo `:37` spreads Axes props only.
- **severity:** low.
- **group:** G2. **priorArt:** NONE. **dependency:** none.

###### C4-C26: CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/context-menu/context-menu-merge.ts:13` `const kept = groups.filter((group) => group.length > 0)` drops only empty groups.
  - `:15-16` `return kept.flatMap((group, index) =>` / `index === 0 ? group : [{ key: \`context-menu-group-${index}\`, separator: true }, ...group],`. A separator-only group, or one with a separator at an edge, yields a doubled, leading or trailing rule.
  - Each separator renders (`packages/ui/src/components/context-menu/context-menu-list.tsx:29`), and the menu kata hides none (`packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/menu.ts:121` `separator: divider.top,`).
- **trigger:** As stated.
- **intent:** context-menu-merge.ts`:4-5` "…dropping empty groups so no menu ever opens on a leading, trailing, or doubled rule." The parameter type admits separators (`:12`; types.ts`:64`).
- **reach:** NONE.
  - `packages/ui/src/modules/grid/grid-context-menu-utilities.tsx:511-524` passes item-only or empty groups.
  - context-menu-merge.ts`:43` passes `ContextMenuItem[]` groups (types.ts`:79`).
  - The grid's hand-placed separators (`packages/ui/src/modules/grid/use-grid-row-manager.tsx:165`, `:188`) do not go through this function.
  - Apps and demos never call it.
- **severity:** low.
- **group:** independent. **priorArt:** NONE. **dependency:** none.

##### §4.2 Scope sheet: C4

###### Groups

**G1 (C4-C02, C4-C18).** A closing modal stays live for its 150 ms exit. Portal's AnimatePresence keeps the open-time tree and its handlers, Overlay adds no exit guard, and neither action handler checks for re-entry. priorArt: NONE (searched exit, double, AnimatePresence, Overlay, onConfirm, onAction).

**G2 (C4-C23, C4-C25).** ContextMenu returns bare children when inactive and Menu's contents `<div>` when active. The tree shape and the `className` target therefore change with `disabled` and with the entry count. priorArt: NONE (D49 is not this seam).

**G3 (C4-C21, C4-C22).** Confirm's header tests truthiness, but its default parameter and the ConfirmBody switch test for `undefined`. It also has no name fallback for a title-less alertdialog. priorArt: NONE.

**G4 (C4-C08, C4-C09, C4-C11).** The filter-change seed is a one-shot write keyed on `deferredQuery`. Under a virtual source it seats index 0 with no enabled check, and on the DOM path it does not scroll. priorArt: NONE.

**G5 (C4-C04, C4-C05).** List emptiness exists only in CSS (`empty:hidden`, `peer-empty:block`, `kara`). No state drives the status text or `aria-expanded`. priorArt: NONE.

**G6 (C4-C14, C4-C15).** `k.item` styles `data-active` only through `hannou.active` (5%). The forced-colors and glass layers key on `:hover`/`:focus`, which a row roved by `aria-activedescendant` never takes. priorArt: NONE.

**Independent:** C4-C01, C4-C03, C4-C06, C4-C10, C4-C12, C4-C13, C4-C16, C4-C17, C4-C19, C4-C20, C4-C24, C4-C26.

###### Ordered steps

Steps run in file order: the item, then Confirm, then the palette root and its state, then the kata, then the context menu. Within a file, the smallest edit goes first, and a gated step comes after the ungated steps in the same file.

- Files: `packages/ui/src/components/command-palette/command-palette-item.tsx`
- Order: 1. This is a one-line edit in the file that S2 and S3 edit next.
- Depends on: none. Gate: none.
- Seam: jsdom, render with `type="submit"` and read `type`.

**S2 (closes C4-C01).**
- Change: cancel the focus default of a pointer press on every option branch (button, link, disabled span) so that focus stays on the input.
- Files: command-palette-item.tsx
- Order: 2. Same JSX as S1.
- Depends on: S1. Gate: none.
- Seam: jsdom, `fireEvent.mouseDown(option)` returns `false`, and `activeElement` stays on the input under a user-event press.

- Files: if Q1 is A, `packages/ui/src/primitives/overlay/overlay.tsx`. If Q1 is B, `packages/ui/src/components/confirm/confirm.tsx` and command-palette-item.tsx (and `packages/ui/src/components/command-palette/context.ts` if the item needs the open state).
- Order: 3. S4 and S5 change the same Confirm handler.

###### Open questions

**Q1 (gates S3).** Where does the guard live?
- (A) In Overlay. One guard covers every modal (Dialog, Sheet, Drawer and their wrappers), written to the node as FloatingSurface does. It also closes the backdrop swallow (L1). Choosing `inert` over `pointer-events: none` also stops keyboard presses, but moves focus out before the exit ends.
- (B) Per component. Confirm and CommandPaletteItem read the live open state through a stable event, and other modals are left as they are.
- What it changes: S3's file set, and whether S4 rides on S3.

**Q2 (gates S5).** Does Confirm take a pending state?
- Add a public pending flag, or keep today's API, where every consumer closes first.
- What it changes: whether S5 runs or drops.

**Q3 (gates S7).** What do falsy `title` and `description` mean?
- A falsy title means "no title" (and needs a new `aria-label` prop), or it falls back to the default.
- `description` set to `null`, `false` or `''` counts as absent (so the children register), or "omitted" keeps meaning `undefined` only (confirm.tsx`:78-79`).
- What it changes: whether S7 adds a public prop.

**Q4 (gates S10).** Shared or palette-only, and which signal?
- Fix `seedVirtualTopMatch`, which also changes Combobox, or fix the palette only.
- Detect a set change with a MutationObserver on the listbox, the identity or count of the registered source, or a key the consumer supplies.
- What it changes: S10's file set and which suites change.

**Q5 (gates S11).** What does Enter do during the deferred lag?
- Ignore it until the deferred commit, flush and run the top match of the input's own query, or keep today's behavior.
- What it changes: whether S11 runs, and what it does.

**Q6 (gates S13).** Where does the composition check go?
- The escape layer, for every dismissable surface, or the palette, by marking the reserved Escape handled.
- What it changes: the blast radius and S13's file.

**Q7 (gates S12).** Where, and how?
- The palette's `onKeyDown`, or the shared `handleActivationKey` (Menu dropdown and Combobox).
- A synthetic click that carries the modifiers, or a navigation through the link component's own path.
- What it changes: S12's file set.

**Q8 (gates S9).** What is the empty-state contract?
- `aria-expanded="false"` on zero results (APG), or `true` with a status message.
- The status text changes inside an always-exposed region, or the CSS-toggled visible text plus a polite `announce`.
- What it changes: S9's markup and the kata `empty` slot.

**Q9 (gates S14).** Palette kata only, or `hannou.active`?
- The Option kata roves by `data-active` too (`packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/option.ts:14`).
- What it changes: S14's file set and the surfaces that change.

**Q10 (gates S16).** What does `className` on ContextMenu style?
- Keep it on the contents wrapper (inherited properties only) and apply it in both states, or render a box for it.
- What it changes: S16's markup.

**Q11 (gates S17).** Does a pointer-opened ContextMenu restore focus?
- Keyboard path only, as the grid does (grid-context-menu.tsx`:172-173`), or both paths.
- What it changes: S17's condition.

###### Ruled-out list

**C4-C07, refuted.**
- Guard: `packages/ui/src/providers/locale/locale.tsx:31-33` "Control strings stay hardcoded English until a second locale lands; a catalog waits for that locale, so its keys answer to a real consumer."
- The trigger is not constructed in the roots: places layout.tsx`:15` and admin layout.tsx`:14` both declare `lang="en"`.

**C4-C01, parts dropped for disabled rows.**
- "Enter or Space activates the clicked row" does not hold for a disabled row. Guard: command-palette-item.tsx`:63-66` `if (disabled) {` / `event.preventDefault()` / `return`. A disabled link renders a `<span>` (`:95-97`), which Enter and Space do not activate.
- "the clicked row paints a `:focus` wash" does not hold for a disabled row. Guard: tint.ts`:13` `not-disabled:not-data-disabled:focus:` against item `:85` `'data-disabled': disabled || undefined,`.

**C4-C06, part dropped.**
- "The focus trap, the focus return … fire on each cycle" is not reached when repeats come faster than the exit.
- The closing surface stays mounted through its exit (portal.tsx`:25` "…kept through its exit animation"; popover.ts`:14` `transition: { duration: duration[150], ease: ease.out },`).
- floating-ui returns focus only in the cleanup of the effect at floating-ui.react.mjs`:2034`, whose dependencies at `:2111` hold no `open`.
- I did not trace how motion's AnimatePresence brings an exiting child back.

###### Surfaced, not judged

- **L1 (cross-component seam, probe).** Every modal built on Overlay keeps its backdrop (overlay.tsx`:179-192`) and panel live for the 150 ms exit, and keeps keyboard focus inside until unmount (floating-ui.react.mjs`:2086`). FloatingSurface already guards its exit (floating-surface.tsx`:101-107`).
- **L2 (cross-component seam, probe).** The exiting AnimatePresence tree runs the handlers it had when open (floating-surface.tsx`:97-99`), so any Dialog consumer's closed-over guard reads open-time state during the exit.
- **L3 (adjacent, sweep).** `IGNORE_NOTHING` also drops tinykeys's `event.isComposing` guard (tinykeys.mjs`:54`), so ⌘K or Ctrl+K pressed during an IME composition toggles the palette.
- **L4 (adjacent, sweep).** use-keybindings.ts`:19-21` documents `() => false` as the recipe and describes the default as a form-field filter only. With tinykeys 4.0.0, that recipe also drops the repeat and composition guards for every caller that follows it.
- **L5 (cross-component seam, probe).** use-escape-layer.ts`:66` has no composition check, so it affects every dismissable surface that holds a text field.
- **L6 (cross-component seam, probe).** `seedVirtualTopMatch` is shared with Combobox (use-a11y-roving.ts`:333-334`). The `isDisabled` gap and the unscrolled DOM seed apply there too.
- **L7 (cross-component seam, probe).** `handleActivationKey` activates by `node.click()` (use-a11y-roving.ts`:683`, `:688`) for every owner that roves by `aria-activedescendant`, so modifiers are lost in the Menu dropdown and Combobox too.
- **L8 (cross-component seam, probe).** `hannou.active` (active.ts`:12`) has no forced-colors or glass mapping, and the Option kata relies on it (option.ts`:14`).
- **L9 (cross-component seam, probe).** The "no results" `<output>` idiom is shared: `packages/ui/src/modules/grid/grid-column-manager.tsx:61-69` names Combobox and CommandPalette.
- **L10 (adjacent gate gap, sweep).** `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts:29` `const CONSUMER_SPREAD = /^(?:props|rest)$/` cannot see `{...forwardedProps(props)}` (command-palette-item.tsx`:114`, `:124`). That is how C4-C03 passed the gate.
- **L11 (adjacent, sweep).** ChartContextMenu's early returns (chart-context-menu.tsx`:431` `if (contextMenu === false) return <>{children}</>`, and `:437`) switch the tree shape the way ContextMenu`:60` does. Toggling a chart's `contextMenu` therefore remounts the chart.

None.

But `:136` `{description && <DialogDescription>{description}</DialogDescription>}` tests truthiness, so `null` registers on neither path. That goes against `:20` "`role="alertdialog"` requires its message referenced by `aria-describedby`." Q3 stays the gate.

So the sheet's "its defaults always hold Download PNG/JPG" holds only while defaults are on. No root passes `defaultItems: false`, so reach does not change (lead L13).

**C4-C25 OVERTURNED → NARROWED.**
- **Source:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/menu/menu.tsx:130-132` "contents: this wrapper must not participate in layout, or it / introduces a box between the trigger/content and whatever flex or / grid container the caller placed the menu in, breaking alignment."
  - `:135` `className={cn('contents', isContextMenu && '[-webkit-touch-callout:none]', className)}`
  - So in the active state the class merges through `cn` onto a wrapper that has no box on purpose. That is what `CONVENTIONS.md:49` asks: "Presentational attributes stay open to override, and `className` merges through `cn`." Only the inactive state breaks the rule.
- **verdict:** NARROWED. Confirmed part: the class is dropped while the menu is inactive. Dropped part: "box utilities paint nothing" while active (added to the ruled-out list in item 2).
- **trigger:** `<ContextMenu className="…">`, then `disabled` toggles or the resolved entries cross zero. context-menu.tsx:60 `if (disabled || entries.length === 0) return <>{children}</>` drops the class, so what it sets goes and comes back with each toggle.
- **intent:** CONVENTIONS.md:49 (quoted above) is broken only by context-menu.tsx:60. menu.tsx:130-132 shows the active wrapper takes no box on purpose. ContextMenuProps `className` (context-menu.tsx:27) has no TSDoc.
- reach, severity, group, priorArt: unchanged (NONE; low; G2; NONE).

**C4-C26 STANDS.**
- context-menu-merge.ts:4-5 promises more than `:13` enforces.
- The one recorded limitation, `:39` "Latent, unreachable today: the separator keys this mints can collide with a", is a different seam.
- No CSS hides an edge rule: `kata/menu.ts:121` `separator: divider.top,` and `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/sen/divider.ts:16` `top: ['border-t', ...tone.borderSubtle],`.

Guard: menu.tsx:130-132 (quoted above) and menu.tsx:135 `className={cn('contents', isContextMenu && '[-webkit-touch-callout:none]', className)}`.
- The C4-C13 reach amendment changes no group, step or question.

##### 3. Surfaced, not judged

Whatever Q8 decides applies there too.

With `defaultItems: false` and a function that returns `[]` for some targets, ContextMenu's entries cross zero between right-clicks, so C4-C23's remount reaches the chart body. No root builds this. I did not trace it past `:426-429`.

### C5 — Card, Code, and CopyButton: blind verdict sheet

Scope C5 blind pass (§3.1–§3.14): Card, Code, CopyButton. Packages/ui C components, 2026-10-04 bug audit.

Of the 20 claims, 17 are CONFIRMED and 3 are NARROWED (C10, C14, C15). None is RESTATED, REFUTED or UNLOCATED. C19 is medium severity; the other 19 are low.

Paths are repo-relative to /home/user/midgard, as the claims write them.

**Input and compliance**
- **Batch check (§2.1):** no claim carries a line number, a trace, a severity or a fix idea, so I accepted the batch.
- **Files not opened:** no sweep evidence, no benchmark, nothing under packages/ui/docs/audits. The bench configs (vitest.bench.*.config.ts) appeared by name in one config Glob and I left them closed.
- **Searches outside the permitted roots:** I ran some to trace mechanisms and the §3.3 resolution surfaces. They were Globs in recipes/kiso/{omote,dan,ji,narabi,iro}, structure/box, components/button, core (announcer*), node_modules/.pnpm (@shikijs), `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-hydrated*`, packages/ui (`*.css`, recursive, paths only) and packages/ui (`{vite,vitest}*.config.*`). They were Greps on single known files: tailwind.css, kata/button.ts, iro/ramp.ts, iro/extended-palette.ts, core/index.ts, components/markdown/markdown-renderer.tsx, five packages/ui configs and @shikijs dist files. None of them listed or opened a benchmark, an audit or sweep evidence.

##### §4.1 Records

###### C5-C01
- **verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - packages/ui/src/components/card/card.tsx:52 `className={cn('overflow-hidden -outline-offset-1', k.base, className)}`
  - card-footer.tsx:16 `export const CardFooter = createSlot('div', 'card-footer', k.footer)`
  - recipes/kata/card.ts:22 `footer: [flex.row, dan.space.box.top, dan.gap.default],`
  - recipes/kiso/narabi/flex.ts:11 `row: 'flex items-center',` (no wrap)
  - recipes/kata/button.ts:23 `'w-fit shrink-0',` (actions do not shrink)
  - card-body.tsx:15 `export const CardBody = createSlot('div', 'card-body')` (no break rule)
  - Overflow from the row or from an unbroken token reaches the card, a hidden-overflow scroll container. The card clips it, gives no user scroll, and still scrolls to a focused descendant.
- **trigger:** as stated. The clip applies to any child of the card, not only CardBody.
- **intent:**
  - card.tsx:26-27: "Outlined, padded surface built on Box."
  - card-footer.tsx:9-10: "Footer region of a {@link Card}, a flex row for actions or supporting controls."
  - No source states clipping or a row that does not wrap as intended, and card.test.tsx has no overflow case.
  - The clip is relied on for media: docs/demos/components/aspect-ratio.tsx:25 `<Card className="p-0">`.
- **reach:** NONE.
  - Searched the five roots for `CardFooter`, `CardBody` and `<Card`.
  - The one footer site, packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/card.tsx:72-75, holds two short actions ("Save changes", "Cancel").
  - The one long-token site guards itself: apps/admin/app/(signed-in)/(dashboard)/users/[userId]/page.tsx:91 `<DescriptionDetails className="font-mono break-all">{user.id}</DescriptionDetails>`.
- **severity:** low. A clipped action costs a lot, but no shipped surface builds the overflow.
- **group:** independent
- **priorArt:** NONE. Searched the digest for Card, overflow, clip, footer, wrap.
- **dependency:** no node_modules line cited.

###### C5-C02
- **verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - code-block.tsx:152 `if (hydrated) html = result?.key === key ? result.html : (htmlCache.get(key) ?? null)`. A cache hit paints but sets no `result`.
  - :165 `if (running.current || htmlCache.has(key)) return`. A cache-hit block runs no pass and stores nothing.
  - :33-36 `if (htmlCache.size >= MAX_CACHE_SIZE) {` / `const first = htmlCache.keys().next().value as string` / `htmlCache.delete(first)`
  - :187 `cacheSet(job.key, markup)` writes one entry for each pass.
  - :204 `}, [key, code, lang, theme])`. The effect does not run again for the same key.
  - After the eviction, the next render finds neither `result` nor the entry, so the block renders the fallback, and no pass runs.
- **trigger:** verified for a block that renders again. In the chat module, the memos stop that render for settled bubbles (see the ruled-out list). The module path is therefore a cache-hit block inside the bubble that streams.
- **intent:**
  - cacheSet TSDoc code-block.tsx:27-28: "a hit doesn't move its entry, so a snippet re-read past 200 distinct others is re-tokenized." The mounted block is not re-tokenized.
  - :112-113: "A cached snippet paints highlighted on the first render of a block".
  - Test code-block.test.tsx:177-192 covers only the first primed paint.
- **reach:** NONE executed.
  - Roots searched for `CodeBlock`, `primeCodeBlock`, `loadShiki`, `Markdown`.
  - No root calls `primeCodeBlock`.
  - Block sites: docs/demos/components/code.tsx:17 and :29; docs/demos/components/markdown.tsx:27 (through components/markdown/markdown-renderer.tsx:179).
  - Chat wiring: modules/chat/chat-message.tsx:38 and :119, chat-tool.tsx:93, all through Markdown. No root consumer streams fenced code (docs/demos/modules/chat.tsx:29-131 holds no fence), and no root adds 200 entries.
- **severity:** low. The highlight is lost until a remount, the code stays readable, and no site builds the sequence.
- **group:** G1
- **priorArt:** NONE. Searched for cache, evict, primeCodeBlock, CodeBlock, Shiki. The nearest entry is DOCS-SITE-AUDIT §9 ruled out, "StrictMode's double render gets the same cached promise." It covers a cached promise under StrictMode, not htmlCache eviction or the early return.
- **dependency:** none.

###### C5-C03
- **verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - The render at code-block.tsx:152 misses the cache and renders the fallback.
  - The passive effect at :165 `if (running.current || htmlCache.has(key)) return` finds the late entry, starts no pass and sets no result.
  - Path 2 writes at :187 `cacheSet(job.key, markup)` from the other block's pass.
  - use-hydrated.ts:15-16: "`false` on the server and in the hydration render, and `true` after. A render that does not hydrate is `true` at once." A client-only mount therefore reads the cache on its first render. A hydrating mount renders again after hydration and heals.
- **trigger:** both paths verified on a client-only mount. Path 2 needs a commit whose passive effects run in a later task (a render outside the sync lane).
- **intent:** primeCodeBlock TSDoc code-block.tsx:81-82: "Call this on the client, before the block renders. A block reads the cache when it renders, so a block that is on the page already keeps its own pass." The block gets no pass at all.
- **reach:** NONE.
  - No root calls `primeCodeBlock`.
  - No root mounts two blocks with the same code, lang and theme: code.tsx:17-25 and :29 differ, and the chat module renders consumer content.
- **severity:** low. The block stays plain until it renders again, and no site builds either path.
- **group:** G1
- **priorArt:** as for C02.
- **dependency:** none.

###### C5-C04
- **verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - code-block.tsx:216 `{html ? (` and :219 `dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: html }}`. Between two cached keys the element stays and only its innerHTML changes.
  - hooks/observe-scroll-extent.ts:19 `resizes.observe(node)`, :21 `for (const child of node.children) resizes.observe(child)`, :34 `mutations.observe(node, { childList: true })`. It does not watch the subtree.
  - kiso/omote/rail.ts:24 `'min-w-0 overflow-x-auto overscroll-x-contain',` and kata/code.ts:31 `content: ['flex-1', ...rail, size.sm],`. Width does not follow content, and the same line count gives the same heights, so no resize fires.
  - use-scroll-region.ts updates only from observeScrollExtent (:124) and font load (:130-132).
  - core/scroll/fade.ts:74 masks only on `'&:is([data-overflow-start], [data-overflow-end])': {`. The fallback at :90 `'@supports not (animation-timeline: scroll())': {` sets :92 `'&[data-overflow-end]': { '--scroll-fade-end': WIDTH },`.
- **trigger:** verified as stated. A cache miss swaps `<div>` and `<pre>`, which is a childList change and re-measures. Precision: if the long variant was scrolled before the switch, a scroll clamp that fires `scroll` re-runs useScrollOverflow (use-scroll-overflow.ts:141 `node.addEventListener('scroll', update, { passive: true })`). useScrollRegion has no scroll listener, so its tab stop and region stay.
- **intent:**
  - code-block.tsx:121-123: "The scroll container is a tab stop only while a line overflows it ({@link useScrollRegion}), and it is then a region named by `label`."
  - use-scroll-region.ts:47-48: "When the content fits again, the hook removes the attributes, so a container that does not scroll adds no empty tab stop."
  - :59-60 lists the triggers: "The state updates on a resize of the node or of its direct children, on a child addition or removal, and after the fonts load." No source accepts the residue in CodeBlock.
- **reach:** NONE. All root sites are static (code.tsx:17 and :29, markdown.tsx:27), and new streamed code misses the cache.
- **severity:** low. Accessibility attributes go stale, but no site builds the switch.
- **group:** independent
- **priorArt:** NONE. Searched for scroll region, tab stop, overflow, fade.
- **dependency:** none.

The packages/ui Vite and Vitest configs have no alias: vitest.browser.config.ts:228-233 is an optimizer include list. ###### C5-C06
- **verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - code-block.tsx:33-39 evicts before `htmlCache.set(key, value)` with no check that the key exists. Setting an existing key keeps the size, so the cache drops to 199.
  - Writers: :94 `cacheSet(cacheKey(code.trim(), lang, theme), html)` and :187 once per pass. Two same-key misses both pass :165 before either pass settles.
- **trigger:** as stated. Edge case: when the existing key is itself the oldest, it is deleted and re-added, and no other entry goes.
- **intent:**
  - code-block.tsx:26-27: "Stores one tokenized snippet, evicting the oldest insertion once the cache is full."
  - :85-86: "The cache holds 200 entries and evicts the oldest entry first". After such a write it holds 199.
- **reach:** NONE. No root calls `primeCodeBlock` and no root mounts a same-key pair.
- **severity:** low. Each duplicate write costs one evicted key; the revert it can cause is G1's mechanism.
- **group:** independent. Its revert consequence closes with S1.
- **priorArt:** as for C02.
- **dependency:** none.

  - code-shiki-highlighter.ts:174 sets `tokenizeTimeLimit: 0`.
  - @shikijs/primitive@4.5.0 dist/index.mjs:621 `const { tokenizeMaxLineLength = 0, tokenizeTimeLimit = 500, includeExplanation = false } = options;` and :637 `if (tokenizeMaxLineLength > 0 && line.length >= tokenizeMaxLineLength) {`. No length cap applies.
  - @shikijs/vscode-textmate@10.0.2 dist/index.js:1813-1820 `while (!STOP) {` / `if (timeLimit !== 0) {` … `scanNext();`. No time cap applies.
  - code-shiki-worker.ts:10-16: one `onmessage` handler on one thread, with a synchronous `codeToHtml`.
  - code-shiki.ts:71-81 `send` sets no timer, and `fail` (:28-36) runs only from `next.onerror` (:59-63).
  - code-block.tsx:165 together with :197-199 `.finally(() => {` … `else run()` means a pending pass blocks the next one. `loadShiki` (code-shiki.ts:117-122) queues behind it too.
  - Confirmed part: while one tokenization runs, a block that needs a pass keeps its fallback, the highlight of a streaming block stops, and `loadShiki` stays pending. Nothing on the main thread times out or restarts the worker.
- **trigger:** as stated. For a backtracking rule, no per-line limit would help, because textmate checks the clock only between scans. The main-thread side is the only bound for that case.
- **intent:**
  - code-shiki-highlighter.ts:162-165: "By default, Shiki stops a line after 500 ms … The worker does not block the page, so it sets no limit, and each line gets all of its tokens."
  - Test code-shiki-highlighter.test.ts:41 `it('gives each token of a line that tokenizes slowly', …`.
  - code-shiki-worker.ts:8-9: "Each request runs on this thread, so a tokenization never blocks the page."
  - No source addresses the queue behind the one worker.
- **reach:** NONE executed. The demo snippets are short (code.tsx:18-24 and :29, markdown.tsx:19-23), and no root gives the chat wiring such a line.
- **severity:** low. Highlighting stalls, but the page and the code stay usable, and no site builds the input.
- **group:** independent
- **priorArt:** NONE. Searched for Shiki, worker, timeout, tokenize. The nearest entry is DOCS-SITE-AUDIT §9 ruled out, "The Shiki warm-up is cancelled cleanly under StrictMode." It covers the warm-up, not a stalled tokenization.
- **dependency:** the entries listed under C05, plus this chain: shiki/package.json:52 → @shikijs/core/package.json:49 `"@shikijs/primitive": "4.5.0"` and :51 `"@shikijs/vscode-textmate": "^10.0.2"`. The store holds only vscode-textmate 10.0.2. A second @shikijs/core@3.23.0 is in the store but not on this chain.

###### C5-C15
- **verdict:** NARROWED
- **mechanism:**
  - use-copy-button-state.ts:75 `const timer = setTimeout(() => {` … :79 `}, timeout)` passes `timeout` through unchecked (copy-button.tsx:65 `timeout = 2000,`).
  - Platform behavior: the timer takes a WebIDL `long`. ±Infinity becomes 0, other values wrap modulo 2^32, and a negative result clamps to 0.
  - Confirmed part: `Infinity`, and values from 2^31 to 2^32−1, revert at once.
  - Dropped part: values of 2^32 and above wrap and do not become 0.
- **trigger:** restated as `timeout={Infinity}`, or a value from 2147483648 to 4294967295.
- **intent:** copy-button.tsx:25-26: "Milliseconds the copied state holds before reverting to the rest glyph. @defaultValue 2000". No range is stated.
- **reach:** NONE. No site passes `timeout=` to CopyButton.
- **severity:** low.
- **group:** independent
- **priorArt:** NONE.
- **dependency:** none.

##### §4.2 Scope sheet

###### Header
Scope C5 covers Card, Code and CopyButton: 19 files, 20 claims.

###### Groups
| Group | Rows | Cause | Prior art |
|---|---|---|---|
| G1 | C02, C03 | A render that paints from htmlCache keeps no copy in `result`, and the effect returns on `htmlCache.has(key)` (code-block.tsx:165). The paint of a cache-hit block therefore follows the cache at each render, and nothing repaints it. | NONE. Searched for cache, evict, prime, effect. |
| G2 | C16, C17 | CardHeader and CardFooter pad their inner edge with no sibling condition (card.ts:21-22). An edge gets two sources when the body is missing or a section sits at an outer edge. | NONE |
| G3 | C12, C14 | useCopyButtonState tracks no write in flight and no unmount. A late resolution flips state and fires callbacks, and an unmount cancels the revert with no transition. | NONE |

###### Ordered steps
Ungated steps come first, then the gated steps by severity. Steps that share a file stay adjacent.

| Step | Rows | Change | Files | Order and dependency | Gate | §10.3 seam |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

The chat path's virtualization is not needed. |

###### Open questions
- **Q-C19: which state does the kata gate the override on?**
  - (a) CopyButton writes a state attribute that the kata reads. This changes the DOM surface of CopyButton.
  - (b) CodeBlock tracks the copied state through onCopiedChange and passes the override only at rest. CopyButton stays unchanged and CodeBlock gains state.
  - (c) Drop the gate. `text-zinc-400` then beats green's light rest shade (code.ts:43-45), so the copied look changes.
- **Q-G3: what does an unmount inside the window owe the consumer?**
  - (a) The cleanup fires `onCopiedChange(false)`, so every true has its false, but a callback runs during unmount.
  - (b) Transitions end at unmount and a late true is dropped, but a true already seen stays.
  - The in-flight guard for C12 runs under either answer.
- **Q-C15: what does a non-finite or over-range `timeout` mean?**
  - (a) Infinity holds the state until unmount.
  - (b) Clamp to 2^31−1.
  - (c) Refuse it in the type or with a warning.
- **Q-C07: who owns a refused copy?**
  - (a) A CodeBlock prop forwards to the button. The Markdown path passes none (markdown-renderer.tsx:179), so chat blocks stay silent.
  - (b) CodeBlock surfaces the failure itself, which reaches every block without a prop.
- **Q-C08: how do blocks get distinct names?**
  - (a) CodeBlock derives a name, for example from `lang`. Two blocks of one language still collide.
  - (b) Markdown names each fence, which touches a file outside the scope.
  - (c) The block takes `labelledBy` from a nearby caption (use-scroll-region.ts:33-37).
- **Q-C09: is the prop surface of CodeBlock open?**
  - (a) Take the `<div>` attributes and spread them on the root in §3.9 order.
  - (b) Stay closed. Hyphenated attributes stay a silent drop.
- **Q-C04: where does the re-measure live?**
  - (a) CodeBlock replaces the markup element when the key changes, so the existing childList observer fires. Only CodeBlock changes.
  - (b) observeScrollExtent also watches the subtree. This touches every caller of useScrollRegion and useScrollOverflow.
- **Q-C05: does `theme` stay open?**
  - (a) Narrow it to the theme or themes the canvas matches.
  - (b) The frame takes the theme background, which Shiki writes on the `<pre>` (core index.mjs:835).
  - (c) Accept a two-tone block for other themes.
- **Q-C20: does CodeBlock get a size axis?**
  - (a) Add `size` as a density scope.
  - (b) Drop `size` from the alias. This changes a public export, so CLAUDE.md §3.1 asks for assent.
- **Q-C10: what bounds a tokenization?**
  - (a) A main-thread timeout for each request that also restarts the worker. It covers long lines and backtracking.
  - (b) `tokenizeMaxLineLength`. It covers long lines only and changes the markup that primeCodeBlock documents (code-block.tsx:71-75).
  - (c) Both.
- **Q-G2: which source owns an edge with no body beside it?**
  - (a) An outer header or footer drops its section pad, and with no body one section owns the shared edge. This follows card.tsx:34-36.
  - (b) A card with only a header, or a header and a footer, is not a supported shape, and consumers use bare content as grid-row-manager.tsx:110-111 does. This follows card-header.tsx:10-12.
- **Q-C01: what does the card do with content wider than itself?**
  - (a) The footer wraps its actions and the body breaks long tokens; the clip stays for media (aspect-ratio.tsx:25 and :40).
  - (b) Use `overflow: clip`. That stops the focus scroll but still hides the content.
  - (c) Both.

###### Ruled-out list
- **C02, trigger example ("parent list re-renders on the next stream chunk"):** settled bubbles skip that render.
  - markdown.tsx:58-61: "Memoized on its (shallow-equal) props … Every earlier, settled bubble's `children` stays the same string."
  - chat-message.tsx:96-97: "Memoized on its (shallow-equal) props, so a transcript's settled bubbles skip re-rendering".
- **C10, dropped parts:**
  - (i) "Every other CodeBlock" is too broad. A block with a `result` or a cache hit paints without the worker: code-block.tsx:152.
  - (ii) "Stops updating" is too broad. The plain text keeps updating: code-block.tsx:222-223 `<pre className={cn(k.block.fallback)} tabIndex={-1}>` / `<code>{code}</code>`.
  - (iii) The per-line no-limit is deliberate: code-shiki-highlighter.ts:164-165 and code-shiki-highlighter.test.ts:41.
- **C14, dropped part (the late announcement):** announcer.ts:47-48 says it is "Use it for one-off, event-driven feedback with no natural focus or DOM home, such as "Copied"". The write did succeed.
- **C15, dropped part:** values of 2^32 and above wrap and do not become 0. No repository line guards any value (use-copy-button-state.ts:75-79).

###### Surfaced, not judged
1. **Adjacent:** card-body.tsx:4, card-description.tsx:5, card-footer.tsx:5 and card-header.tsx:5 say the slot "accepts `render` for composition". createSlot (core/create-slot.ts:22-27) spreads every prop onto an intrinsic element and handles no `render`.
2. **Adjacent, root of C05:** in a non-default theme, a scrolled overflowing line leaves the `<pre>` box. Its text then sits on the #0d1117 frame.
3. **Cross-component seam:** markdown-renderer.tsx:179 keys each block by token index. An index shift while a message streams remounts the block, which then paints from the cache with no `result`. This is G1's entry path.
4. **Cross-component seam:** observeScrollExtent (observe-scroll-extent.ts:28-34) watches only direct children. Every caller of useScrollRegion or useScrollOverflow whose content changes inside a stable child keeps stale attributes.
5. **Cross-component seam:** the demo smoke `landmark-unique` gate (demo-pages.tsx:94) runs in jsdom with no layout (demo-smoke.tsx:24). No region that exists only on overflow (CodeBlock, Table, ScrollArea, PdfViewer) ever appears to it.
6. **Adjacent (app):** apps/admin/app/(signed-in)/account/two-step.tsx:130 and :232 copy the TOTP key and the recovery codes with no `onCopyError`. A refused write is silent (copy-button.tsx:35-39).
7. **Cross-component seam for S16:** modules/dashboard/dashboard-tile-header.tsx:103 `<div data-slot="card-header" className={cn(k.header)}>` reuses the anchor. A G2 fix that selects `card-header` also matches the dashboard tile header.

###### UNLOCATED
None.

### C5: overturn report

Overturn pass, scope C5 (Card, Code, CopyButton), audit of 2026-10-04.

All 20 verdicts stand. Nothing proves any claim deliberate or already known. Three fields change: the C12 reach, the C15 trigger, and the C15 ruled-out entry. The S3 precondition is now met.

Before I cited `node_modules`, I read the resolution surfaces (see C19).

**C5-C15 — STANDS (NARROWED); trigger and dropped part amended.**
- Source: the sheet's own rule at C5.md:290: "±Infinity becomes 0, other values wrap modulo 2^32, and a negative result clamps to 0."
- By that rule, 4294967296 wraps to 0. A value whose remainder modulo 2^32 is 2^31 or more wraps to a negative value and clamps to 0. Both revert at once, so "values of 2^32 and above wrap and do not become 0" is too broad.
- trigger, restated in full: `timeout={Infinity}`, or a value whose integer part modulo 2^32 is 0 or at least 2^31. The first band is 2147483648 to 4294967296, and each later band has the same form (for example, 6442450944 to 8589934592).
- verdict, intent, reach, severity, group and priorArt do not change.

**Ruled-out list: the C15 entry is replaced.** A value of 2^32 or more whose remainder modulo 2^32 is 1 to 2147483647 holds the copied state for that remainder in milliseconds, not 0. No repository line guards any value (use-copy-button-state.ts:75-79).

1. **Cross-component seam (the C11 family).** Two other inline marks also write `data-density` beside their children and open no context:

### C6 — Checkbox, Collapse, and Control: blind verdict sheet

**C6 blind pass: verdict sheet for Checkbox, Collapse and Control**

10 of the 12 claims are CONFIRMED and 2 are REFUTED (C6-C04 and C6-C10). No claim is NARROWED, RESTATED or UNLOCATED. C6-C11 is medium. Every other confirmed claim is low.

Input check: I accepted the batch. The 12 claims carry no line number, no code trace, no severity and no fix idea. All paths below are absolute.

##### §4.1 Claim records

###### C6-C01 — CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - The Checkbox never asks for `readOnly`. `packages/ui/src/components/checkbox/checkbox.tsx:57-63` reads "} = useControlProps({ / id, / disabled, / required, / 'aria-describedby': ariaDescribedBy, / invalid, / })".
  - The destructure (`checkbox.tsx:29-44`) leaves an explicit `readOnly` in `:43` "...props".
  - So it never reads the resolved value at `packages/ui/src/components/control/use-control-props.ts:91` "readOnly: input.readOnly ?? control?.readOnly,".
  - The cascade does reach the field:
    - `packages/ui/src/components/control/control.tsx:72` "const mergedReadOnly = readOnly || parent?.readOnly".
    - `packages/ui/src/components/control/use-control-field-context.ts:52` "readOnly: parent?.readOnly," (the CheckboxField envelope).
  - An explicit prop lands on the native input: `checkbox.tsx:98` "{...props}" with `:99` "type="checkbox"". The native `readonly` attribute has no effect on a checkbox.
  - `checkbox.tsx:88-115` writes no `aria-readonly` and adds no guard against activation.
  - A bound form stores the toggle: `packages/ui/src/components/form/use-form-toggle.ts:50` "field?.setValue(event.target.checked)".
- **trigger:** as stated.
- **intent:**
  - `control.tsx:39-43`: "broadcasts `autoComplete`, `disabled`, `readOnly`, `required`, `severity`, and `variant` / to control-aware descendants. Those are input, textarea, switch, listbox, / combobox, datepicker, checkbox, and radio. Nests: `disabled` / `readOnly` / cascade through inner Controls".
  - `packages/ui/src/components/control/context.ts:54-55`: "Read by input, textarea, switch, listbox, combobox, datepicker, checkbox, / radio."
  - No source exempts the checkbox. The readOnly tests cover Input only: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/control.test.tsx:152-160` and `:306-316`.
- **reach:** NONE.
  - Searched all five roots for `readOnly`. The hits are Rating, PasswordInput, Input, CreditCardInput, the query chips and the grid column types only.
  - Searched for `<Checkbox\b`: 11 sites. None passes `readOnly`, and none sits under a read-only Control.
  - No shipped default sets it.
- **severity:** low. The cost would be medium (a read-only form lets the box change and submits the change), but no shipped surface builds the trigger.
- **group:** G1
- **priorArt:** NONE. I searched the digest for checkbox, readOnly, read-only, Control and cascade.
- **dependency:** none.

###### C6-C02 — CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - `checkbox.tsx:33` "indeterminate," is destructured, so the input markup carries no indeterminate signal.
  - The only writer is a layout effect, which does not run in a server render. `checkbox.tsx:69-72`: "// `indeterminate` is a DOM property with no attribute; sync it before paint. / useLayoutEffect(() => { / if (internalRef.current) internalRef.current.indeterminate = !!indeterminate / }, [indeterminate])".
  - The fill and the glyph key on `:indeterminate`, which only that property sets:
    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/checkbox.ts:21`: "'has-[:indeterminate]:bg-(--check-bg) has-[:indeterminate]:border-(--check-border)',"
    - `:40-41`: "'pointer-events-none absolute stroke-(--check-mark) opacity-0', / '[:checked~&]:opacity-100 [:indeterminate~&]:opacity-100',"
  - So before hydration an unchecked box paints empty.
  - With `checked`, `checkbox.tsx:86` "const Mark = indeterminate ? Minus : Check" and `:106` "checked={resolvedChecked}" show the minus glyph. The native state is still plain "checked".
- **trigger:** as stated.
- **intent:**
  - `checkbox.tsx:14`: "Renders the partial tri-state: a minus glyph and `indeterminate` DOM property regardless of `checked`."
  - `:69` says "sync it before paint". On a server render the first paint is the server HTML, which the effect cannot reach.
  - The tests cover the client render only: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/checkbox.test.tsx:58-75`.
- **reach:**
  - `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/checkbox.tsx:9` "const [selected, setSelected] = useState&lt;readonly string[]>(['Cheese'])" and `:52-54` "&lt;Checkbox / checked={selected.length === toppings.length} / indeterminate={selected.length > 0 && selected.length &lt; toppings.length}". The box is indeterminate and unchecked on the first render.
  - The docs site is prerendered and then hydrated:
    - `packages/ui/src/docs/react-router.config.ts:20-21`: "// A static site: no server at run time. The build renders each page to its own / // HTML file, and the browser hydrates it."
    - `:26-27`: "ssr: false, / prerender: () => ['/', ...pagePaths()],"
  - Not traced: whether the lazy demo chunk lands in the prerendered HTML.
  - The other indeterminate site is `packages/ui/src/modules/grid/grid-static-header-cell.tsx:60` "indeterminate={someSelected && !allSelected}". It starts unselected in every root:
    - The demo selections start as "new Set()": `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/modules/grid/index.tsx:441`, `:814`, `:1035`, `:1221` and `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/modules/grid/editable.tsx:758`.
    - The app grids define no selection.
- **severity:** low. One docs demo is affected, and the defect heals when hydration runs the layout effect.
- **group:** independent
- **priorArt:** NONE. I searched the digest for indeterminate, hydration, SSR and Checkbox.
- **dependency:** none.

- **severity:** low. No shipped surface passes either prop, and the cost is a misplaced style.
- **group:** G1

###### C6-C05 — CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - The first column is fixed. `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/narabi/toggle.ts:15-16`: "'group/field grid grid-cols-[1.125rem_1fr]', / 'gap-x-3',".
  - Nothing sets `justify-self`. `toggle.ts:22`: "'*:data-[slot=control]:col-start-1 *:data-[slot=control]:row-start-1 *:data-[slot=control]:self-center',".
  - The wiring:
    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/toggle.ts:6` "field: toggle,"
    - `packages/ui/src/primitives/toggle/toggle-field.tsx:13`
    - `packages/ui/src/components/checkbox/checkbox-field.tsx:23` "&lt;ToggleField className={cn(k.disabled, className)} {...props} />"
  - The box steps with the density scope:
    - `kata/checkbox.ts:24-25` "// The box takes the step of the nearest density scope. / checkbox.box,"
    - which leads to `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/checkbox.ts:17` "const box = dan.size.check.box"
    - which leads to `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/dan/size.ts:39` "box: 'density-size-[4,5,6]',"
  - The utility: `packages/ui/src/core/density/utilities.ts:8` "Three values give `sm`, `md`, and `lg`" and `:51-52` "calc(var(--spacing) * ${stop}…)".
  - Result: the box is 1rem, 1.25rem or 1.5rem in a 1.125rem column. At md it overruns the column by 2px, at lg by 6px, and at sm it falls 2px short.
  - The space before the label is therefore 10, 6 or 14px, against `gap-x-3` (12px).
  - The overrun goes toward the inline end, so in RTL it also goes toward the label.
- **trigger:** as stated.
- **intent:**
  - `narabi/toggle.ts:2-5`: "the two-column grid that holds a check/radio/switch / control alongside its label and description. Control sits in column 1 / row 1, label in column 2 row 1".
  - No source fixes the column to a box step.
  - The geometry test covers message placement only: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/geometry/toggle-field-message.test.tsx:7-11`.
- **reach:**
  - `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/checkbox.tsx:18-19` "&lt;CheckboxField> / &lt;Checkbox {...props} defaultChecked />". This is the Axes demo; its size axis comes from the docs engine, which I did not trace.
  - The same file at `:26-27`, `:35-36`, `:41-42`, `:51-52`, `:61-62` and `:80-81`.
  - `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/form.tsx:336-337` "&lt;CheckboxField> / &lt;Checkbox name="terms" />" and `:343-344`.
  - `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/confirm.tsx:64-65`.
  - `packages/ui/src/modules/grid/grid-manager-checkbox-row.tsx:31-32` "&lt;CheckboxField> / &lt;Checkbox {...checkbox} />". Its callers are `grid-column-manager.tsx:292` and `:347`, and `grid-group-manager.tsx:594` and `:680`.
  - All of these render at md unless an outer scope sets another step.
- **severity:** low. The cost is spacing drift with no overlap at any step, but every CheckboxField shows it.
- **group:** independent
- **priorArt:** NONE. I searched the digest for toggle, grid column, CheckboxField, density and gap.
  - D42 shares the `confirm.tsx:65` site but records a different defect: "| D42 | … confirm.tsx:65 | … Confirm toggles from the closure. | … setAccepted(e.target.checked). | low | R | Open |".
- **dependency:** none.
  - The default `--spacing` is assumed. `packages/ui/tailwind.css:1-14` sets none.
  - I did not read the app entry stylesheets.

###### C6-C06 — CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**
  - The kata's checked border, `kata/checkbox.ts:20-21`: "'has-checked:bg-(--check-bg) has-checked:border-(--check-border)', / 'has-[:indeterminate]:bg-(--check-bg) has-[:indeterminate]:border-(--check-border)',".
  - The check surface's neutral hover border, joined into the same box through `katakana/control.ts:97`:
    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/control/check.ts:48`: "'not-has-[:disabled]:hover:border-zinc-950/70 not-has-[:disabled]:group-has-[[data-slot=label]:hover]/field:border-zinc-950/70',"
    - `:53`: "'dark:not-has-[:disabled]:hover:border-white/50 dark:not-has-[:disabled]:group-has-[[data-slot=label]:hover]/field:border-white/50',"
  - Specificity:
    - `has-checked` is a class plus `:has(*:checked)`: (0,2,0).
    - The box hover is a class plus `:not(:has(:disabled))` plus `:hover`: (0,3,0).
    - The label hover adds `:is(:where(.group/field):has([data-slot=label]:hover) *)`: (0,4,0).
    - `dark:` adds nothing above that.
  - So the neutral hover border wins in both modes, whatever the source order.
  - The intended checked-hover feedback is the separate rule at `kata/checkbox.ts:22-23` "'not-has-[:disabled]:has-checked:hover:opacity-90',".
- **trigger:** as stated. A hover on the box alone is enough, so the bare grid checkboxes reach it too.
- **intent:**

###### C6-C08 — CONFIRMED
- **mechanism:**

    - `check.ts:33` "'has-[[data-invalid]]:ring-2 has-[[data-invalid]]:ring-red-600',"

  - Precision on the sticky part: a sticky child is not clipped. The panel becomes its scrollport, so the child stops sticking to the page.

- **reach:**

    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/markdown.ts:29-31`: "so the prose sits flush in its container … '[&>:last-child]:mb-0'"

###### C6-C12 — CONFIRMED
The zero height follows from the CSS Flexbox rules; I did not observe it in a browser.
- **mechanism:**
  - The skeleton picks the joined shape inside any group:
    - `packages/ui/src/components/control/control-skeleton.tsx:36` "const joined = dataGroup !== undefined".
    - `control-skeleton.tsx:43` "className={cn(k.skeleton.base, joined ? k.skeleton.group : k.skeleton.full, className)}".
  - The joined shape is `flex-1` plus a min-width:
    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/control.ts:24` "base: [rounded.lg, dan.size.control.base]," and `:26` "group: `flex-1 ${dan.size.control.min}`,".
    - `dan/size.ts:83` "base: 'density-h-[7.5,9.5,11.5]'," and `:85` "min: 'density-min-w-[16,24,32]',".
  - A vertical Group is an inline-flex column. `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/group.ts:16`: "vertical: ['inline-flex flex-col [--touch-target-gap-y:0px]', ...tsunagi.vertical].join(' '),".
  - The vertical join classes set no flex value: `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/tsunagi.ts:58-73`, ending at `:73` "'[&>[data-group-orientation=vertical]]:w-auto',".
  - The placeholder is an empty block: `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/placeholder.ts:9` "base: [skeleton.base, 'block density-any:h-4', rounded.lg],".
  - Height: in a column of auto height, `flex-1` gives a basis of 0% against a main size with no fixed value. That basis resolves to the content size, which is 0 for an empty block. The automatic minimum is the smaller of 0 and the specified height, so it is also 0.
  - Width: the members stretch to the max-content width of the inline-flex group, and only the min-width supplies that.
- **trigger:** as stated.
- **intent:**
  - `control-skeleton.tsx:24-26`: "A skeleton inside a group / therefore draws the joined shape, and one outside draws the full one."
  - `kokkaku/control.ts:7-8`: "`group` / is the in-group default: grows to share the row with sibling / placeholders".
  - Vertical groups are meant to work: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/control-skeleton.test.tsx:41-52` "it('forwards the stamp, so the join selectors reach the placeholder' … &lt;Group orientation="vertical">".
- **reach:** NONE.
  - Searched all five roots for `ControlSkeleton`.
  - `apps/places/src/components/place-filters/place-filters.tsx:32` "loading: () => &lt;ControlSkeleton />," has no Group.
  - `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/primitives/skeleton.tsx:71`, `:161` and `:164` have no Group.
  - The shipped FiltersSkeleton, which `place-filters.tsx:206` uses, wraps it in a Flex, not a Group: `packages/ui/src/components/filters/filters-skeleton.tsx:45-57`.
  - Test-only site: `control-skeleton.test.tsx:41-53`.
- **severity:** low. The cost is the shape of a loading placeholder, and no consumer builds the trigger.
- **group:** independent
- **priorArt:** NONE. I searched the digest for skeleton, Group, vertical, flex-1 and silhouette.
- **dependency:** none.

##### §4.2 Scope sheet

**Scope:** C6 (Checkbox, Collapse, Control)

###### Groups
- **G1:** Checkbox resolves a fixed set of props (`checkbox.tsx:29-44`, `:57-63`) and spreads the rest onto the invisible overlay input (`checkbox.tsx:98`). A cascade value it does not request (`readOnly`) and presentational props (`hidden`, `style`) therefore never act on the visible box. Members: C6-C01, C6-C03.
- **Independent:** C6-C02, C6-C05, C6-C06, C6-C07, C6-C08, C6-C09, C6-C11, C6-C12.

###### Ordered steps
None of these defects sits under floating-ui, pdfjs, fetch or virtualization. Each step still names the synchronous seam a test can drive.

**S1 — closes C6-C11**
- **Change:** make the Label's `for` and the control's id agree when a control under a field wrapper takes an explicit id, by the means Q1 picks.
- **Files, by Q1 outcome:**
  - (a) `packages/ui/src/components/control/context.ts`, `packages/ui/src/components/control/control.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components/control/use-control-field-context.ts`, `packages/ui/src/components/control/use-control-props.ts`, `packages/ui/src/components/fieldset/label.tsx`.
  - (b) every control that calls `useControlProps`: `packages/ui/src/components/checkbox/checkbox.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components/input/input.tsx`, and the other readers listed at `context.ts:54-55`.
  - (c) `use-control-props.ts` only.
  - Tests: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/control.test.tsx` and `checkbox.test.tsx`.
- **Order:** 1, because it is the only medium.
- **Depends on:** none.
- **Gate:** Q1.
- **Seam:** jsdom, with `getByRole('checkbox', { name })` and a click on the label.

**S2 — closes C6-C06**
- **Change:** stop the neutral hover border from replacing the checked or indeterminate border.
- **Files, by Q2 outcome:**
  - (a) `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/control/check.ts`
  - (b) `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/checkbox.ts`
  - Test: a new file under `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/`, modeled on `control-validation-hover.test.tsx`. It compares the computed border color before and after a hover on the box and on the label, in both modes.
- **Order:** 2, because it has the widest shipped reach and is one class change with a proven test pattern.
- **Depends on:** none.
- **Gate:** Q2.
- **Seam:** the browser suite (`userEvent.hover` and `getComputedStyle`). jsdom loads no stylesheet.

**S3 — closes C6-C05**
- **Change:** size the first column of the toggle grid from the box step, not from a fixed 1.125rem.
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/narabi/toggle.ts`. Under Q3(b), add `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/dan/size.ts`.
  - Test: under `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/geometry/`. The gap from box to label at sm, md and lg, in LTR and RTL.
- **Order:** 3, because every CheckboxField at md shows it.
- **Depends on:** none.
- **Gate:** Q3.
- **Seam:** the geometry instance of the browser suite (`getBoundingClientRect`).

**S4 — closes C6-C08**
- **Change:** keep the clip only while the height animates. No clip at rest after an open lands, and none for `animate={false}`.
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/collapse.ts` and `packages/ui/src/components/collapse/collapse-panel.tsx`. Under Q4(a), add `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/ugoki/collapse.ts`.
  - Tests: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/collapse.test.tsx`, plus a browser case for an outline at the panel edge.
- **Order:** 4. One shipped module site.
- **Depends on:** none.
- **Gate:** Q4.
- **Seam:** the global motion mock, which lands on a change of animate target (`collapse.test.tsx:351-353`). This is synchronous in jsdom.

**S5 — closes C6-C07**
- **Change:** key the trigger's open color on the trigger's own state, in place of the any-ancestor `group-data-[open]/collapse` match. The trigger already carries `aria-expanded`: `use-a11y-disclosure.ts:81` sets it, through `collapse.tsx:83` and `collapse-trigger.tsx:32`.
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/collapse.ts`, plus a browser test.
- **Order:** 5, because its reach is NONE.
- **Depends on:** S4, which edits the same file.
- **Gate:** none.
- **Seam:** the browser suite (computed color of the inner trigger). jsdom can only assert the attribute.

**S6 — closes C6-C02**
- **Change:** put the indeterminate state into the server HTML.
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/checkbox/checkbox.tsx` and `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/checkbox.ts`.
  - Test: `checkbox.test.tsx`, using `renderToString`.

- **Depends on:** S1 under Q1(b), which edits the same file.

**S7 — closes C6-C01 and C6-C03 (G1)**
- **Change:**
  - Resolve `readOnly` through `useControlProps` and apply the read-only behavior from Q6. Place it after the spread, per CONVENTIONS §3.9.

**S8 — closes C6-C09**
- **Change:** emit `aria-controls` only when its target id is in the same HTML.
- **Files, by Q8 outcome:**
  - (a) or (b): `packages/ui/src/components/collapse/collapse-trigger.tsx`, and also `collapse-panel.tsx` under (b).
  - (c): `packages/ui/src/primitives/mount/mount.ts` and its other callers (lead L4).
  - Test: a `renderToString` case in `collapse.test.tsx`, next to the existing client case at `:242-253`.
- **Order:** 8, because its reach is NONE.
- **Depends on:** none.
- **Gate:** Q8.
- **Seam:** `renderToString`.

**S9 — closes C6-C12**
- **Change:** make the joined shape aware of the group's axis. `flex-1` applies along a horizontal group only. A member of a vertical group keeps the control height and takes the group width.
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/control.ts`, plus a browser geometry test.
- **Order:** 9. Its reach is NONE, and it affects a loading state only.
- **Depends on:** none.
- **Gate:** none.
- **Seam:** the geometry instance of the browser suite. jsdom computes no layout.

###### Open questions

**Q1 (S1): how do the label and the control agree?**
- (a) The control reports its resolved id to the context, and the Label reads it. The label works after mount, but the server HTML keeps the mismatch.
- (b) Each control also applies the context's labelledBy. The accessible name survives an id override, but a click on the label still does nothing. This touches every control and puts `checkbox.tsx` in S1's file set.
- (c) A development warning steers the consumer to `htmlFor` on the wrapper. There is no runtime fix.

**Q2 (S2): where does the hover fix go?**
- (a) In the shared check surface (`check.ts:48`, `:53`). This fixes Radio too (lead L3) and needs a Radio assertion.
- (b) In the checkbox kata only. Radio keeps the seam.
- Also decide whether a checked box shows any hover change beyond the `opacity-90` at `kata/checkbox.ts:22-23`.

**Q3 (S3): what is the column rule?**
- (a) An `auto` first column. It follows any box, Radio included, and the label offset changes with each step.
- (b) A fixed column per step that tracks `dan.size.check.box`. This needs a new ramp and keeps one offset per step.
- The outcome also decides what the geometry test asserts.

**Q4 (S4): how does the clip end?**
- (a) Clip only during the height transition, and clip again before a close starts. This closes every part of C6-C08.
- (b) Keep the clip and mark the panel as a `[data-scroll-region]`. This fixes only the controls that use `focus.outline` (Checkbox). Button's `focus.ring`, the rings, the shadows and sticky children stay affected.

**Q5 (S6): what does the server HTML carry?**
- (a) A data attribute that the kata keys the fill and the glyph on. This fixes the paint, but assistive tech reads the native state until hydration.
- (b) Also `aria-checked="mixed"` on the native input. This fixes assistive tech before hydration, but goes against ARIA-in-HTML guidance for `input type=checkbox`.
- (c) Accept the window until hydration.

**Q6 (S7, C6-C01): what does read-only mean for a checkbox?**
- (a) Block activation and set `aria-readonly`. The box keeps focus and submits.
- (b) Treat it as `disabled`: no focus and no submission.
- (c) The checkbox stops being a reader of `readOnly` from the cascade. That is a contract change, not a code fix.
- Also decide whether Switch moves in the same step (lead L1).

**Q7 (S7, C6-C03): which attributes move to the visible box?**
- (a) `style` and `hidden`.
- (b) A named set of presentational attributes.
- (c) None. Keep the routing to the input and narrow `CheckboxProps` instead.
- Also decide whether Switch follows (lead L2).

**Q8 (S8): how does the reference stay correct in the server HTML?**
- (a) Leave `aria-controls` out of the server output and the hydration pass, and add it afterwards through a client flag that is safe for hydration.
- (b) Server-render the closed panel outside the hidden Activity. This changes the tree shape between server and client.
- (c) Answer it in `primitives/mount` for every held panel (lead L4).

**C6-C06 — STANDS. The trace holds over the caller check.**
- **Selector shapes, now quoted from the resolved build.** From `node_modules/.pnpm/tailwindcss@4.3.0/node_modules/tailwindcss/dist/lib.mjs:16`:
  - `has`: "p.selector=`&:has(${p.selector.replaceAll("&","*")})`"
  - `not`: "`&:not(${L(f,",").map(d=>(d=d.replaceAll("&","*"),d)).join(", ")})`"
  - `checked`: "r("checked",["&:checked"])"
  - `hover`: "i.static("hover",f=>{f.nodes=[q("&:hover",[W("@media","(hover: hover)",f.nodes)])]})"
- So the checked border is `.x:has(*:checked)` at (0,2,0), and the neutral hover is `.x:not(*:has(:disabled)):hover` at (0,3,0). Source order cannot change the result.
- **This suite applies that shape on `userEvent.hover`.** `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/control-validation-hover.test.tsx:48` "it('still darkens the ring of a control with no validation state'" runs against `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kasane/layers.ts:45` "'not-has-[>:disabled]:not-has-[[data-invalid]]:…:hover:ring-zinc-400'".
