##### §4.1 Records

**C7-C01: CONFIRMED** (group G1, priorArt NONE (P))
- **Mechanism:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:80-87` passes neither `meaningful` nor `atEnd`, so `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:35` `const defaultMeaningful = (c: string) => /[A-Za-z0-9+]/.test(c)` and `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:51` `atEnd = 'restore',` apply.
  - On "/" after "4": `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:63` `const meaningfulBefore = countMeaningful(raw, cursor, meaningful)` gives 1. `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-utilities.ts:87` `const ONE_DIGIT_MONTH = /^\D*([1-9])(?=\D)/` and `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-utilities.ts:95` `const d = digitsOnly(raw.replace(ONE_DIGIT_MONTH, '0$1')).slice(0, 4)` give "04/".
  - `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:67` `setCaret(cursorForCount(formatted, meaningfulBefore, meaningful))` with `packages/ui/src/utilities/caret.ts:29` `if (count === target) return i + 1` gives caret 1. `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts:40` `useLayoutEffect(flush)` and `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts:35` `el.setSelectionRange(target, target)` apply it.
  - "2" gives "02/4". "7" gives "02/74".
  - "(invalid)": `node_modules/.pnpm/card-validator@10.0.4/node_modules/card-validator/dist/expiration-year.js:46-48` `valid = numericValue >= twoDigitYear && numericValue <= twoDigitYear + maxElapsedYear;` and 74 > 26+19. `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:127` `setTypedInvalid(next.length === EXPIRY_PATTERN.length && !validity.isValid)` then marks the field.
- **Trigger:** as stated (desktop). A typed space takes the same path (`packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/credit-card-input-utilities.test.ts:113` `expect(formatExpiry('1 26')).toBe('01/26')`).
- **Intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-utilities.ts:91-92`: "A one-digit month that a typed separator follows gets a leading zero, so `4/27` masks to `04/27`."
  - `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:21-23`: "A padding formatter breaks the `meaningful` contract … Each pad inserts a meaningful character, and the restore then pins the caret one place short."
  - `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:25`: "Masks must keep `'restore'`".
  - No source accepts the misplaced caret. `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/credit-card-input-utilities.test.ts:106-114` tests whole strings only.
- **Reach:** `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/credit-card-input.tsx:50` `<CreditCardInputExpiry />`.
- **Severity:** medium. A common way to type an expiry produces a wrong, invalid entry, on a shipped demo.
- **Dependency:** `node_modules/.pnpm/card-validator@10.0.4/node_modules/card-validator/dist/expiration-year.js` under R.

**C7-C02: CONFIRMED** (G1, NONE (P))
- **Mechanism:**
  - After "12", the caret sits at 2 (`packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:67`, `packages/ui/src/utilities/caret.ts:29`).
  - Backspace leaves raw "1/". `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:166` `if (maskedValue.endsWith('/') && raw === maskedValue.slice(0, -1)) {` is false, so `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:176` `onMaskedChange(event)` runs. `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-utilities.ts:95` pads "1/" to "01/".
  - `packages/ui/src/components/mask-input/use-mask-input.ts:65` `onValueChange: onChange ? (v) => onChange(v ?? '') : undefined,` fires `onValueChange("01/")`. The caret goes to 1.
  - A second Backspace gives raw "1/" with the cursor at 0, which formats to "01/" again. `packages/ui/src/utilities/caret.ts:21` `if (target <= 0) return 0` and `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts:51` `if (position !== null) queueMicrotask(flush)` pin the caret at 0.
- **Trigger:** as stated.
- **Intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:45-46`: "masks digits into "MM/YY", auto-inserting the slash and handling backspace across it".
  - `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:164-165`: "backspace over it deletes the preceding digit instead".
  - `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:25-26`: "a caret before trailing separators is what makes backspace work".
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/credit-card-input.test.tsx:154-167` covers only the caret-after-slash path: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/credit-card-input.test.tsx:165` `await user.type(input, '{Backspace}')` re-enters through user-event's click, which I did not run.
- **Reach:** `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/credit-card-input.tsx:50`.
- **Severity:** medium. The most common correction corrupts the month and traps the caret.
- **Dependency:** none.

##### §4.2 Scope sheet: C7

###### Groups
- **G1** (C01, C02, C03): CreditCardInputExpiry infers the edit from the resulting text. The one-digit-month pad (`packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-utilities.ts:87`, :95) fires on deletions as well as typed separators, and it inserts a meaningful "0" that the caret restore does not count. The slash guard (`packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:166`) treats every removal of the trailing slash as a Backspace.
- **G2** (C07, C17, C20, C28): the caret's `meaningful` set is wider than the set the format keeps. The masks use the default alphanumeric predicate (`packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:35`) and keep digits only. CurrencyInput counts every "-" (`packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input-utilities.ts:28`) but keeps only a leading one (`packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input-utilities.ts:70-72`).
- **G3** (C04, C16, C19): the trio has no single default-name policy. Expiry and `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx` default an `aria-label` that yields only to a Field Label, so it outranks a native label. CreditCardInput defaults nothing, so its placeholder becomes the name.
- **G4** (C05, C06): the expiry's built-in error Message (`packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:185`) borrows the Field's single error slot for its id and its wiring (`packages/ui/src/components/fieldset/message.tsx:61`, :109). Outside a Field it has neither. Inside a Field it shares the slot id with any other error Message.
- **G5** (C22, C23, C24): the currency editor's symbols do not come from the text it reads back. The decimal falls back to "." when no decimal part is sampled (`packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/use-currency-input-formatting.ts:71`). Symbols come from the currency style, while the display and the editing use the decimal style. The locale minus sign is not an editor character.
- **Independent:** C09, C10, C11, C12, C13, C14, C27, C29, C30, C31, C32, C33.
- **PriorArt for every group:** NONE (search P).

###### Ordered steps
Ungated steps come first; same-file steps come in merge order. No step sits on floating-ui, pdfjs, fetch or virtualization, so §10.3 bars no test here.

- **S1, G1 (closes C01, C02, C03), first: the highest cost in the scope, on a shipped demo.**
  - **Change:** decide the pad and the slash handling from the kind of edit. Pad a one-digit month only on an insertion that ends in a separator, and put the caret after the padded month. Delete the digit before the slash only on a backward delete that removes the trailing "/".
  - **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-utilities.ts`, and `packages/ui/src/components/mask-input/use-mask-input.ts` only if the caret offset or `atEnd` must pass through the hook. Tests: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/credit-card-input.test.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/credit-card-input-utilities.test.ts`.
  - **Gate:** none.
  - **Test seam:** `formatExpiry` is pure. The edit runs in jsdom with user-event after `setSelectionRange(2, 2)`, as `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/currency-input.test.tsx:121-123` does.
- **S2, G5 (closes C22, C23, C24), second: medium cost; its files do not overlap S1's, so it can run beside it.**
  - **Change:**
    - Take group, decimal and minus from the formatter style whose text the editor reads.
    - Sample the decimal with at least one fraction digit.
    - Map U+2212 to "-" before the `packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input-utilities.ts:25` filter.
  - **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/use-currency-input-formatting.ts`, `packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input-utilities.ts`, and `packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input.tsx` if the display formatter changes. Tests: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/use-currency-input-formatting.test.ts`, `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/currency-input-utilities.test.ts`, `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/currency-input.test.tsx`.
  - **Gate:** Q9, for the C23 part only. The C22 and C24 parts can land before it.
  - **Test seam:** `renderHook(useCurrencyInputFormatting)` and the pure `formatEditing`/`parseEditing`. The assertions read ICU data.
- **S3, G2 (closes C07, C17, C20, C28), after S1 (`packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx`) and S2 (`packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input-utilities.ts`).**
  - **Change:** pass a digits-only `meaningful` to the three `useMaskInput` calls (`packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input.tsx:85`, `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:80`, `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx:77`). Make CurrencyInput count "-" only where `formatEditing` keeps it.
  - **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input.tsx` and `packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input-utilities.ts`; `packages/ui/src/utilities/caret.ts` and `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts` also under Q2(a). Tests: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/credit-card-input.test.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/currency-input.test.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/currency-input-utilities.test.ts`.
  - **Gate:** Q2, for the C28 half only. The mask half can land first.
  - **Test seam:** the pure `packages/ui/src/utilities/caret.ts` functions, plus user-event with `setSelectionRange`.
- **S4, G4 (closes C05, C06), after S3 (same file).**
  - **Change:** give the built-in Message an id of its own, and put that id in the input's `aria-describedby` from the component.
  - **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/credit-card-input.test.tsx`.
  - **Gate:** none.
  - **Test seam:** jsdom accessible-description assertions.
- **S5, C13, after S3 (`packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx`).**
  - **Change:** the re-fit compares the cap with the value the parent holds, and in controlled mode it reports the truncation through `onValueChange`.
  - **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx`, and `packages/ui/src/components/mask-input/use-mask-input.ts` if the unformatted controlled value must be exposed. Test: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/credit-card-input.test.tsx`.
  - **Gate:** none (`packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx:47-49` decides).
  - **Test seam:** a jsdom rerender, as in `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/credit-card-input.test.tsx:353-366`.

###### Open questions
- **Q1 (gates S11):** how does a default name yield to a name it cannot see at render, such as a native `<label for>`?
  - (a) Read `input.labels` after mount: the attribute then changes after hydration.
  - (b) Treat a passed `id` as the sign of an outside label: a heuristic, with no DOM read.
  - (c) Keep the current yield set and only add CreditCardInput's default: this closes C19 only.
- **Q2 (gates the C28 half of S3):**
  - (a) A position-aware `meaningful` in `packages/ui/src/utilities/caret.ts` and `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts`, shared with DateInput and the masks.
  - (b) A "-" anywhere negates the value and moves to the front: no hook change, but the behavior changes.
- **Q3 (gates S13):** is typed input of non-ASCII digits in scope?
  - (a) Map the digits in `packages/ui/src/utilities/digits-only.ts`: this reaches every caller of that shared utility, which this pass did not list.
  - (b) Map them in the card formatters only.
  - (c) Leave it out of scope: C12 then stands by decision.
  - Under (a), CurrencyInput's ASCII-only parser (C25's basis) is a separate decision.
- **Q4 (gates S12):**
  - (a) Pass `luhnValidateUnionPay: true`: typos are caught, but real UnionPay numbers that do not use Luhn are rejected.
  - (b) Keep the dependency default: `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-utilities.ts:117`'s "Luhn checksum" then does not describe UnionPay.
- **Q5 (gates S15):**
  - (a) Accept "," or "." as the decimal when the mark can only be a decimal (for example, the last mark with at most `maxFractionDigits` digits after it): `formatEditing`'s contract and its tests change.
  - (b) Keep strict pinning: C27 stands by decision.
- **Q6 (gates S14):** does the buffer yield to an unchanged controlled value that differs from the buffer's parse?
  - (a) In the controlled path only: `packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input.tsx:132-133`'s bound-field lag does not apply there.
  - (b) In all paths: this needs another answer to that lag.
  - Also decide what the field shows after a rejected keystroke.
- **Q7 (gates S16):** what locale applies when none is set, so that server and client agree?
  - (a) A fixed fallback.
  - (b) A source that both sides read, such as the document `lang`.
  - (c) Settle after hydration: the page changes visibly.
  - All three change `packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input.tsx:25`'s contract and probably `resolveLocale` (lead L4).
- **Q8 (gates S17):**
  - (a) No change: no root takes negative amounts.
  - (b) An opt-in that selects a keypad with "-": a public props change.
- **Q9 (gates S2's C23 part):** which style supplies the separators?
  - (a) The currency style for display, editing and parsing: fr-CH "1 234.50", de-AT "1.234,50".
  - (b) The decimal style for all three: fr-CH "1 234,50", de-AT "1 234,50".

###### Leads (surfaced, not judged)
- **L1 (adjacent defect, the G1 mechanism; route to a sweep):** `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-utilities.ts:87` pads on any non-digit after a leading single digit. A stray letter in "12/2" after the "1" gives "01/22".
- **L2 (cross-component seam; route to a probe):** `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts:34-35` calls `setSelectionRange` with no guard for input types that do not support selection. `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts:21-23` allows `type` overrides, so any `useFormattedInput` consumer whose type is overridden that way throws InvalidStateError on the first restore.
- **L3 (cross-component seam; route to a probe):** any two error Messages in one Field share the same id (`packages/ui/src/components/fieldset/message.tsx:61`) and one ref-counted registration (`packages/ui/src/hooks/a11y/use-a11y-scope.ts:115`, :120).
- **L4 (cross-component seam; route to a probe):** `resolveLocale` (`resolve-locale.ts:11`, `:21`) and every Intl call with an undefined locale fall back to the runtime default, which the docs prerender fixes at build time. Other format-aware components on prerendered pages may mismatch as C32 does. Not checked.
- **L5 (adjacent API seam; route to a sweep):** the three card props types omit `type` (`packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:18-21`, `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx:22-25`, `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input.tsx:21-24`), yet `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts:21-23` calls `type="password"` on a `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx` legitimate. A consumer can reach that override only through a cast spread.

  - `packages/ui/src/docs/engine/README.md:142-145`: "(`ssr: false`) … The build renders each path to its own HTML file, and the browser hydrates it."
  - `packages/ui/src/docs/react-router.config.ts:26-27`: `ssr: false,` `prerender: () => ['/', ...pagePaths()],`.
  - `providers/locale/context.ts:6-9` and `packages/ui/REFERENCE.md:37` do not record that server and client can disagree.
- **C33: STANDS.** I rechecked `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/currency-input.test.tsx:25` and `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/currency-input-utilities.test.ts:53`. Q8 decides it.

###### New records for C08, C18 and C21

**Shared reason:** `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts:21-23` says "`type` on the input family stays out of scope. §3.9 names `type` for the form-submit hazard, which is a button concern, so `type="password"` on a `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx` field is a legitimate override." That keeps the spread order deliberate, but it also makes a crash under such an override a defect. The throw is part of each claim's own wrong result, so I judge it here instead of leaving it as lead L2. §3.14 leads are only for what no claim carries.

**C7-C08: NARROWED** (group G6, priorArt NONE)
- **Mechanism:** the throw happens in this order.
  - `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:135` `type="text"` comes before `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:146` `{...props}`.
  - `packages/ui/src/components/mask-input/use-mask-input.ts:68` `const { ref, reformat } = useFormattedInput({ format, meaningful, ref: externalRef })` passes no `atEnd`, so `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:51` `atEnd = 'restore',` applies.
  - For an input type outside JSD:45 `new Set(["text", "search", "url", "tel", "password"])`, JSD:629-630 returns null for `selectionStart`. `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:59` `const cursor = event.target.selectionStart ?? raw.length` then falls back, and `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:67` queues a caret restore.
  - `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts:34-35` `if (el && document.activeElement === el) {` `el.setSelectionRange(target, target)` then reaches JSD:677-678 `if (!this._idlMemberApplies("setSelectionRange")) {` `throw DOMException.create(… "InvalidStateError"])`.
  - The first keystroke changes the value, so the throw comes from `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts:40` `useLayoutEffect(flush)`.
- **Trigger:** a spread that carries an input type with no text selection (`number`, `email`, a date type), then the first keystroke. `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts`'s own example, `password`, is in JSD:45 and does not throw. Dropped part: the replacement of `type` and `inputMode`, and the "MM/YY" text that then cannot render (see the ruled-out list).
- **Intent:** `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts:21-23`, quoted above. `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts:7` "Caret-preserving plumbing for formatted text inputs." names the hook's domain but does not accept a throw. `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:59` already handles a null `selectionStart` where it reads the caret. No source accepts the throw.
- **Reach:** NONE. `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/credit-card-input.tsx`, read in full, passes no `type` to the expiry (`packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/credit-card-input.tsx:50`), and `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:18-21` omits `type` from the props. Test-only: none. No `__tests__` file passes such a type to the three card fields, and `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/credit-card-input.test.tsx:389` `const stray = { onChange: vi.fn() } as object` carries no `type`.
- **Severity:** low. A keystroke throws InvalidStateError during the commit, but only when a spread supplies a type without selection, and no root does that.
- **Group:** G6.
- **PriorArt:** NONE. No row in the four open audits names `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts`, `setSelectionRange` or a `type` override. HCP:110 records only the `onChange` fix.
- **Dependency:** JSD under R2. A browser throws the same way under the HTML spec, which is a platform fact.

**C7-C18: NARROWED** (G6, NONE). Same as C08, except:
- **Mechanism:** `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx:118` `type="text"` comes before `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx:128` `{...props}`, and `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx:77` calls into `packages/ui/src/components/mask-input/use-mask-input.ts:68`.
- **Intent:** `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts:21-23` names this exact field.
- **Cost:** `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx` values are digits only, so the field would display under `type="number"`. Here the throw is the whole cost.
- **Reach:** NONE. `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/credit-card-input.tsx:54` `<CreditCardInputCvv brand={brand} />` passes no type, and `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx:22-25` omits `type`.
- **Severity:** low, for the same reason as C08. **Dependency:** JSD under R2.

**C7-C21: NARROWED** (G6, NONE). Same as C08, except:
- **Mechanism:** `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input.tsx:109` `type="text"` comes before `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input.tsx:118` `{...props}`, and `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input.tsx:85` calls into `packages/ui/src/components/mask-input/use-mask-input.ts:68`.
- **Reach:** NONE. `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/credit-card-input.tsx:23`, :38, :75, :79, :83, :95 and :102 pass no `type`. The Axes spread at `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/credit-card-input.tsx:68` carries only literal-union props (`docs/engine/axes.ts:116` `const values = literalsOf(prop.type)`), and `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input.tsx:21-24` omits `type`.
- **Severity:** low, for the same reason as C08. **Dependency:** JSD under R2.

##### 2. Changes to the sheet

- **Header:** use the counts given at the top.
- **Groups:** add **G6** (C08, C18, C21). Cause: the masks always use `'restore'` (`packages/ui/src/components/mask-input/use-mask-input.ts:68`), and the restore calls `setSelectionRange` without checking that the input type supports selection (`packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts:34-35`). A `type` that `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts:21-23` leaves open to the spread therefore throws on the first keystroke. PriorArt: NONE.
- **Steps:** add **S18, G6, closing C08, C18 and C21.**
  - **Change:** the restore skips an input whose `selectionStart` is null, which is the same signal `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:59` already reads. `type` stays before the spread.
  - **Files:** `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts` and T-UPC. `usePendingCaret` is public (`packages/ui/docs/HOOKS.md:89`), so its TSDoc line `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts:11` ("Restores only while the input holds focus.") changes with it, per CLAUDE.md §3.4.
  - **Order:** independent. No other step touches `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts` or T-UPC, so it can run beside S1.
  - **Gate:** none.
  - **Test seam:** synchronous jsdom. T-UPC's `setup()` with `input.type = 'number'` throws today at JSD:677-678. §10.3 bars nothing here.
  - The same guard also fixes the MaskInput case left in L2.
- **Ruled-out list:** the C08, C18 and C21 entry now covers only the replacement part. Its quotes stay: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts:21-23` "… `type="password"` on a `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx` field is a legitimate override", and `CONVENTIONS.md:49` lists no `inputMode`. The C30 entry is unchanged.
- **Open questions:** no change. No other group or step changes.

##### 3. Leads (surfaced, not judged)

- **L2, narrowed by G6:** what remains is MaskInput. It keeps `type` as a public prop (MI:49 `type,`, MI:80 `type={type ?? preset.type}`) on the same `'restore'` path (MI:66 `} = useMaskInput({`). So `<MaskInput type="number">` throws without any spread. S18 would fix it too. Consumers that use `'jump'` (`packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input.tsx:153`, DI:249) return at `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:61` and do not throw. This is outside C7.
- **L5, amended:** MI:92-93 says "The props type omits `onChange`, but an untyped spread can still pass one." By the package's own reading, an untyped spread can carry an omitted key without a cast. That applies to the omitted `type` on the card fields and to C30's `onChange`.
- **L6, new (cross-component seam):** the default-label logic in C04 and C16 also appears in other components:
  - DI:283 `aria-label={ariaLabel ?? (control?.labelledBy ? undefined : 'Date')}`
  - `components/address-input/address-input.tsx:189` `aria-label={ariaLabel ?? (control?.labelledBy ? undefined : placeholder)}`
  - `components/tag-input/tag-input.tsx:298` `aria-label={ariaLabel ?? (control?.labelledBy ? undefined : (placeholder ?? 'Add tags'))}`

  Q1's answer therefore affects these files, not only the three card fields.
- **L7, new (cross-component seam; the sweep's C12 and C25 evidence names it):** `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:56-70` reformats every change event with no IME composition check. `packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input.tsx:172` `if (event.key === 'Enter' && !isComposing(event)) event.currentTarget.blur()` checks composition only for Enter. Every mask and CurrencyInput can therefore rewrite the value while an IME is still composing.
- L1, L3 and L4 are unchanged.
